## What the runner is
`src/pilot_file_drat_v5.py` (SHA-256 b1400f67cc2268bbb572dbe24d3b379b40682fffb0961af460e504a9ced483a2) is a research harness. It runs same-binary controls C1-C7 and then three SAT items, one native child at a time:
- The items are independent-encoder CNFs for a combinatorial-design case split. The encoder is `src/vendor/encode_skel_b.py`, and the expected CNF digests are in `evidence/cnf-pins-v1.json`.
- Solver: kissat 4.0.4, `kissat -q -f --seed=1 --time=600 CNF PROOF_FIFO`. The binary DRAT proof goes into a lane-local FIFO, and an owned sink thread writes it to a proof FILE with a hard cap.
- Checker: `drat-trim CNF PROOF_FILE -i -t 1200` (source in `checker/drat-trim.c`).
- An item may be recorded UNSAT and counted only if `qualify_unsat()` and `terminal_gate()` both pass.

The previous version `src/pilot_file_drat_v3.py` received NO-GO. The exact diff is `src/pilot-runner-v3-to-v5.diff`. Parent findings on v3:
- **P1.** The stability parser rejected the real monitor stream, whose first line is an `observer_start` event; see `control/resource-observer-live-v3-first40.jsonl` and `control/resource_observer_v3.py`. Only the explicit known event schema may be allowed, remaining lines must be strict samples, and the window must be `[time.time()-300, time.time()]`.
- **P2.** v3 could publish UNSAT and count=true after a cancel was latched during or after the checker. A final per-item consumer gate is required before any counted status.
- **P3.** The process status was ambiguous: exit 0 on failures. Explicit nonzero outcomes are required.
- **P4.** The proof cap was only a sampled file size with an assumed overshoot. A hard byte bound, or an honestly scoped soft cap, is required.

## Binding rules the runner must obey (see `control/launch-contract-tightening-v1.json`)
- No SIGKILL, no subprocess timeouts, no ulimit, no deletion or overwrite, no reading of old FIFOs.
- The only allowed signal is a SIGTERM to an owned, identity-safe child, followed by a bounded wait. A survivor must be quarantined (recorded, capacity held), never escalated.
- **Chunk fit:** child end ≤ min(now + 10800 − 120, settled_by − 180), plus 120 s settlement and a 60 s margin.
- **Stability rule** (`control/resource-stability-addendum-v1.json`, monitor path from v2): ≥25 samples spanning ≥290 s in the last 300 s; last sample age ≤30 s; last free ≥ 13.5e9 + projected bytes; max peak-to-later drop ≤ max(3e8, (last_free − 13.5e9)//12); timestamps timezone-aware and strictly increasing; any malformed complete line refuses.
- **Fresh statvfs:** free ≥ 12.2e9 + projected growth, and free − remaining reservations ≥ 12e9.
- **STOP registry:** refuse on any `STOP*`/`HALT*`/`SAT-CANDIDATE*` marker in the lane or run control directories, or on any change to the nine inherited old markers pinned in `evidence/inherited-stop-registry-v1.json`.
- **Streamed proofs:** producer-read and checker-consumed byte counts plus SHA boundaries must be stated exactly.
- **Settled** means: the direct child is reaped, its process group is empty, the streams are closed, the artifacts are hashed, and the receipt is written. Otherwise the run is UNKNOWN or UNSETTLED.

## Review tasks, in priority order
1. **Capped-output overshoot.** Is the proof file truly hard-bounded by `PROOF_CAP`? Are the stdout/stderr capture files truly hard-bounded by `LOG_CAP`? Can any path write beyond them or block a producer forever, for example when a FIFO pipe fills after the cap or the drain stops? Are the reservations (`ITEM_RESERVE`, `CONTROL_RESERVE`) consistent with all retained outputs, including control files?
2. **Post-Popen exception settlement.** Check every exception path after a child or sink exists: Child init, `supervise`, `run_child`, `run_kissat` and `ProofSink.finish`. Can any path orphan a child, leave a FIFO reader thread blocked, signal a reaped or reused PID, signal more than once, or mark something settled when it is not?
3. **Proof-file immutability and consumed count.** Check the FIFO sink semantics, `sink_complete()`, the drat-trim byte count `S->nReads` (`checker/drat-trim.c`, around `read_lit` and `parse`), and the pre/post hashes. Does "read N bytes from proof file" equal the file length for a complete binary proof? Can a truncated or partial proof or CNF still pass? Check the early-EOF behavior (`parse()` early EOF break, formula initialization) against the predicate.
4. **Terminal consumer verdict.** Can any path write `status=UNSAT` or `count_for_scientific_coverage=true` without every predicate and the terminal gate passing, including races with SIGTERM/cancel, registry changes and disk floor? Check the summary and exit-code semantics.
5. **Stability-parser correctness** against the real monitor format and the rule above. Also check the chunk-fit arithmetic and the exit codes.
6. **Controls C1-C7.** Does each control demonstrate its intended reason with the same binaries? Could a control pass vacuously?
7. **Assumptions about kissat behaviour** (the binary is not in the snapshot). Examples: rc 20/10/0, `-q` still printing status lines, `-f` allowing a FIFO proof path, a binary proof by default, SIGTERM handling. Flag every assumption that makes a predicate fail-open; assumptions that only cause fail-closed outcomes are fine.

Return JSON matching the provided schema. Use `severity`=`blocker` only for issues that could produce a wrong UNSAT/count, an orphaned or unsignalled survivor, an unbounded write, or a rule violation.

   481	
   482	def run_child(argv, out_dir, label, wall, sink=None):
   483	    ch = Child(argv, os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".out"), LOG_CAP)
   484	    try:
   485	        if CANCEL.is_set():
   486	            ch.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
   487	        trip, peak = supervise(ch, wall, sink)
   488	    except BaseException as exc:
   489	        ch.term("runner_exception:%r" % exc)
   490	        trip, peak = ch.stop, None
   491	    finally:
   492	        rec = ch.settle(out_dir, label)
   493	    rec["trip"] = trip
   494	    rec["peak_sampled_lane_rss_bytes"] = peak
   495	    return rec, segments(ch.out_path)
   496	
   497	
   498	def run_kissat(cnf_path, proof_file, out_dir, label, seconds, proof_cap=PROOF_CAP):
   499	    """kissat with its proof routed through a hard-capped owned sink; proof_file None = no proof."""
   500	    argv = [KISSAT[0], "-q", "-f", "--seed=1", "--time=%d" % seconds, cnf_path]
   501	    sink = None
   502	    if proof_file is not None:
   503	        fifo_dir = os.path.join(out_dir, "fifo")
   504	        os.makedirs(fifo_dir, exist_ok=True)
   505	        sink = ProofSink(os.path.join(fifo_dir, label + ".proof.fifo"), proof_file, proof_cap)
   506	        argv.append(sink.fifo_path)
   507	    try:
   508	        rec, lines = run_child(argv, out_dir, label + ".kissat", seconds, sink)
   509	    finally:
   510	        sink_rec = sink.finish() if sink is not None else None
   511	    rec["proof_sink"] = sink_rec
   512	    return rec, lines
   513	
   514	
   515	def run_drat(cnf_path, proof_path, out_dir, label, seconds):
   516	    return run_child([DRAT, cnf_path, proof_path, "-i", "-t", str(seconds)], out_dir, label + ".drat", seconds)
   517	
   518	
   519	def sink_complete(sink_rec):
   520	    return (sink_rec is not None and sink_rec["connected"] and sink_rec["eof"] and not sink_rec["over_cap"]
   521	            and sink_rec["error"] is None and not sink_rec["unblocked_by_runner"]
   522	            and not sink_rec["thread_alive_after_join"] and sink_rec["discarded"] == 0
   523	            and sink_rec["received"] == sink_rec["written"]
   524	            and sink_rec.get("file_rehash") == [sink_rec["sha256_of_written"], sink_rec["written"]])
   525	
   526	
   527	def qualify_unsat(cnf_expect, solver_rec, kissat_lines, proof_sha, proof_bytes, drat_rec, drat_lines,
   528	                  cnf_post, proof_post):
   529	    bad = []
   530	    if solver_rec["returncode"] != 20 or solver_rec["stop"] != "self" or solver_rec["trip"] is not None:
   531	        bad.append("solver not self-exit rc20")
   532	    if [s for s in kissat_lines if s.startswith("s ")] != ["s UNSATISFIABLE"]:
   533	        bad.append("solver status lines not exactly ['s UNSATISFIABLE']")
   534	    if solver_rec.get("proof_sink") is not None and not sink_complete(solver_rec["proof_sink"]):
   535	        bad.append("proof sink incomplete/over cap/error")
   536	    if proof_bytes <= 0 or proof_bytes > PROOF_CAP:
   537	        bad.append("proof size outside (0, cap]")
   538	    if drat_rec["returncode"] != 0 or drat_rec["stop"] != "self" or drat_rec["trip"] is not None:
   539	        bad.append("checker not self-exit rc0")
   540	    st = [s for s in drat_lines if s.startswith("s ")]
   541	    if st != ["s VERIFIED"]:
   542	        bad.append("checker status lines %r" % st)
   543	    v, c = cnf_expect[2].split()[2:4]
   544	    if ("c parsing input formula with %s variables and %s clauses" % (v, c)) not in drat_lines:
   545	        bad.append("checker parse header line missing/mismatched")
   546	    if ("c finished parsing, read %d bytes from proof file" % proof_bytes) not in drat_lines:
   547	        bad.append("checker-consumed proof bytes != proof file bytes")
   548	    for pat in ("early EOF", "clauses less than expected", "trivial UNSAT", "ERROR", "MEMOUT",
   549	                "wrong binary prefix", "no empty clause detected", "NOT VERIFIED", "TIMEOUT"):
   550	        if any(pat in s for s in drat_lines):
   551	            bad.append("checker output contains %r" % pat)
   552	    if cnf_post != (cnf_expect[0], cnf_expect[1]):
   553	        bad.append("CNF file changed during run")
   554	    if proof_post != (proof_sha, proof_bytes):
   555	        bad.append("proof file changed during check")
   556	    for rec in (solver_rec, drat_rec):
   557	        if rec["group_empty_after_reap"] is not True or rec["quarantine_record"] or rec["reader_error"] \
   558	                or rec["capture_over_cap"]:
   559	            bad.append("settlement/capture defect pid %s" % rec["pid"])
   560	    return bad
   561	
   562	
   563	def terminal_gate():
   564	    """P2: final consumer gate evaluated immediately before a counted status is written."""
   565	    why = []
   566	    if CANCEL.is_set():
   567	        why.append("cancel latched")
   568	    try:
   569	        why += registry_refusals()
   570	        if free_bytes() < FREE_FLOOR:
   571	            why.append("free below in-flight floor at terminal gate")
   572	    except Exception as exc:
   573	        why.append("terminal gate exception %r" % exc)
   574	    return why
   575	
   576	
   577	def verify_sat(cnf_path, kissat_lines, out_dir, label):
   578	    """Two independent checks of a SAT model: clause satisfaction and both PA19 definitions."""
   579	    lits = set()
   580	    for s in kissat_lines:
   581	        if s.startswith("v "):
   582	            lits.update(int(x) for x in s[2:].split() if x != "0")
   583	    sat_all = True
   584	    with open(cnf_path, "rb") as f:
   585	        for ln in f:
   586	            if ln.startswith(b"p") or ln.startswith(b"c"):
   587	                continue
   588	            cl = [int(x) for x in ln.split()[:-1]]
   589	            if not any(l in lits for l in cl):
   590	                sat_all = False
   591	                break
   592	    A = [[None] * 8 for _ in range(19)]
   593	    cells_ok = True
   594	    for r in range(19):
   595	        for c in range(8):
   596	            ss = [s for s in range(6) if (1 + 48 * r + 6 * c + s) in lits]
   597	            if len(ss) != 1:
   598	                cells_ok = False
   599	            else:
   600	                A[r][c] = ss[0]
   601	    rowpair_ok = cells_ok and all(sum(A[r][c] == A[q][c] for c in range(8)) <= 1
   602	                                  for r in range(19) for q in range(r + 1, 19))
   603	    colpair_ok = cells_ok and all(len({(A[r][c], A[r][d]) for r in range(19)}) == 19
   604	                                  for c in range(8) for d in range(c + 1, 8))
   605	    cand = os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".SAT-CANDIDATE.json")
   606	    write_x(cand, {"host_clock": clock(), "array": A, "clauses_all_satisfied": sat_all,
   607	                   "cells_exactly_one": cells_ok, "row_pair_agreements_le_1": rowpair_ok,
   608	                   "column_pair_ordered_pairs_distinct": colpair_ok,
   609	                   "status": "CANDIDATE_UNVERIFIED - notify parent immediately"})
   610	    latch = os.path.join(LANE, "control", "SAT-CANDIDATE-" + label)
   611	    write_bytes_x(latch, cand.encode())
   612	    return {"candidate": cand, "latch": latch, "clauses_all_satisfied": sat_all, "cells": cells_ok,
   613	            "rowpair": rowpair_ok, "colpair": colpair_ok}
   614	
   615	
   616	def php_cnf(p, h):
   617	    """Pigeonhole: p pigeons into h holes; UNSAT iff p > h."""
   618	    var = lambda i, j: i * h + j + 1  # noqa: E731
   619	    cl = [[var(i, j) for j in range(h)] for i in range(p)]
   620	    for j in range(h):
   621	        for i in range(p):
   622	            for k in range(i + 1, p):
   623	                cl.append([-var(i, j), -var(k, j)])
   624	    return ("p cnf %d %d\n" % (p * h, len(cl)) + "".join(" ".join(map(str, c)) + " 0\n" for c in cl)).encode()
   625	
   626	
   627	def controls(out_dir):
   628	    """Same-binary positive and intended-reason mutation controls; returns (ok, records)."""
   629	    recs, ok = {}, True
   630	    d = os.path.join(out_dir, "controls")
   631	    os.mkdir(d)
   632	    raw = php_cnf(6, 5)
   633	    cnf = os.path.join(d, "php6x5.cnf")
   634	    h, n = write_bytes_x(cnf, raw)
   635	    exp = (h, n, raw.split(b"\n", 1)[0].decode())
   636	    proof = os.path.join(d, "php6x5.drat")
   637	    ks, kl = run_kissat(cnf, proof, d, "C1", 60)
   638	    ph, pn = sha_file(proof)
   639	    dr, dl = run_drat(cnf, proof, d, "C1", 60)
   640	    c1 = qualify_unsat(exp, ks, kl, ph, pn, dr, dl, sha_file(cnf), sha_file(proof))
   641	    recs["C1_positive_php6x5"] = {"intended": "PASS (sink complete, consumed bytes equal)", "defects": c1,
   642	                                  "kissat": ks, "drat": dr}
   643	    ok &= (c1 == [])
   644	    # C2: truncated CNF (last clause dropped, header unchanged) + valid proof -> early-EOF rejection.
   645	    trunc = raw[: raw.rstrip(b"\n").rfind(b"\n") + 1]
   646	    cnf2 = os.path.join(d, "php6x5-truncated.cnf")
   647	    write_bytes_x(cnf2, trunc)
   648	    dr2, dl2 = run_drat(cnf2, proof, d, "C2", 60)
   649	    c2 = qualify_unsat(exp, ks, kl, ph, pn, dr2, dl2, (h, n), (ph, pn))
   650	    recs["C2_truncated_cnf"] = {"intended": "REJECT: early EOF or proof never consumed", "defects": c2, "drat": dr2}
   651	    ok &= any(("early EOF" in x) or ("clauses less than expected" in x) or ("consumed" in x) for x in c2)
   652	    # C3: proof with one trailing garbage byte -> consumed bytes != file bytes (wrong binary prefix).
   653	    with open(proof, "rb") as f:
   654	        pbytes = f.read()
   655	    proof3 = os.path.join(d, "php6x5-trailing.drat")
   656	    p3h, p3n = write_bytes_x(proof3, pbytes + b"x")
   657	    dr3, dl3 = run_drat(cnf, proof3, d, "C3", 60)
   658	    c3 = qualify_unsat(exp, ks, kl, p3h, p3n, dr3, dl3, (h, n), (p3h, p3n))
   659	    recs["C3_trailing_proof_byte"] = {"intended": "REJECT: consumed bytes / binary prefix", "defects": c3, "drat": dr3}
   660	    ok &= any(("consumed" in x) or ("wrong binary prefix" in x) for x in c3)
   661	    # C4: satisfiable formula (PHP 5x5) checked against the PHP 6x5 proof -> rejection.
   662	    raw4 = php_cnf(5, 5)
   663	    cnf4 = os.path.join(d, "php5x5-sat.cnf")
   664	    h4, n4 = write_bytes_x(cnf4, raw4)
   665	    exp4 = (h4, n4, raw4.split(b"\n", 1)[0].decode())
   666	    dr4, dl4 = run_drat(cnf4, proof, d, "C4", 60)
   667	    c4 = qualify_unsat(exp4, ks, kl, ph, pn, dr4, dl4, (h4, n4), (ph, pn))
   668	    recs["C4_sat_formula_foreign_proof"] = {"intended": "REJECT", "defects": c4, "drat": dr4}
   669	    ok &= (c4 != [])
   670	    # C5: HARD proof cap 4096 B on a hard instance -> sink never writes beyond 4096, trip proof_cap, settled.
   671	    cnf5 = os.path.join(d, "php11x10.cnf")
   672	    write_bytes_x(cnf5, php_cnf(11, 10))
   673	    proof5 = os.path.join(d, "php11x10.drat")
   674	    ks5, kl5 = run_kissat(cnf5, proof5, d, "C5", 60, proof_cap=4096)
   675	    size5 = os.lstat(proof5).st_size
   676	    recs["C5_proof_hard_cap"] = {"intended": "trip=proof_cap, file<=4096 B, reaped, group empty, no quarantine",
   677	                                 "kissat": ks5, "proof_file_bytes": size5}
   678	    ok &= (ks5["trip"] == "proof_cap" and size5 <= 4096 and ks5["group_empty_after_reap"] is True
   679	           and not ks5["quarantine_record"] and ks5["proof_sink"]["over_cap"] is True
   680	           and not ks5["proof_sink"]["thread_alive_after_join"])
   681	    # C6: native solver time limit, no proof file -> self exit without an UNSAT verdict.
   682	    cnf6 = os.path.join(d, "php13x12.cnf")
   683	    write_bytes_x(cnf6, php_cnf(13, 12))
   684	    ks6, kl6 = run_kissat(cnf6, None, d, "C6", 1)
   685	    recs["C6_native_time_limit"] = {"intended": "self exit, no s UNSATISFIABLE", "kissat": ks6,
   686	                                    "status_lines": [s for s in kl6 if s.startswith("s ")]}
   687	    ok &= (ks6["trip"] is None and ks6["returncode"] != 20 and ks6["group_empty_after_reap"] is True
   688	           and "s UNSATISFIABLE" not in kl6)
   689	    # C7: unreadable input (path does not exist) with a proof sink -> no verdict, sink settles, no hang.
   690	    ks7, kl7 = run_kissat(os.path.join(d, "absent.cnf"), os.path.join(d, "absent.drat"), d, "C7", 10)
   691	    recs["C7_no_input_sink_liveness"] = {"intended": "no rc20, reaped, sink thread finished", "kissat": ks7}
   692	    ok &= (ks7["returncode"] != 20 and ks7["group_empty_after_reap"] is True
   693	           and not ks7["proof_sink"]["thread_alive_after_join"])
   694	    return ok, recs
   695	
   696	
   697	def main():
   698	    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, on_term)
   699	    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, on_term)
   700	    if len(sys.argv) != 4:

 succeeded in 0ms:
   701	        print("usage: pilot_file_drat_v5.py GO.json GO_SHA256 OUT_DIR", flush=True)
   702	        return EXIT_PRESTART_REFUSED
   703	    go_path, go_sha, out_dir = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], os.path.abspath(sys.argv[3])
   704	    if not out_dir.startswith(LANE + "/evidence/pilot-v1/"):
   705	        print("REFUSED: output outside LANE/evidence/pilot-v1/", flush=True)
   706	        return EXIT_PRESTART_REFUSED
   707	    self_sha = sha_file(os.path.abspath(__file__))[0]
   708	    try:
   709	        go = json.loads(read_pinned(go_path, go_sha))
   710	    except Exception as exc:
   711	        print("REFUSED: GO unreadable/pin mismatch %r" % exc, flush=True)
   712	        return EXIT_PRESTART_REFUSED
   713	    if go.get("runner_sha256") != self_sha or go.get("decision") != "GO" \
   714	            or go.get("items") != [it["id"] for it in ITEMS]:
   715	        print("REFUSED: GO record does not name this runner/items", flush=True)
   716	        return EXIT_PRESTART_REFUSED
   717	    t_start = time.time()
   718	    abs_end = t_start + CONTROLS_WORST_SECONDS + len(ITEMS) * ITEM_WORST_SECONDS
   719	    if abs_end + SETTLE + MARGIN > t_start + MAX_CHUNK or abs_end > OWNED_SETTLED_BY - 180:
   720	        print("REFUSED: chunk-fit", flush=True)
   721	        return EXIT_PRESTART_REFUSED
   722	    os.mkdir(out_dir)
   723	    reserve_total = CONTROL_RESERVE + len(ITEMS) * ITEM_RESERVE
   724	    launch = {
   725	        "schema": "PA19-96H-PILOT-LAUNCH-v5", "host_clock": clock(), "owner": "ContinuePA19",
   726	        "go": {"path": go_path, "sha256": go_sha}, "runner": {"path": os.path.abspath(__file__), "sha256": self_sha},
   727	        "pid": os.getpid(), "ppid": os.getppid(), "pgid": os.getpgid(0), "identity": identity(os.getpid()),
   728	        "cwd": os.getcwd(), "argv": sys.argv, "python": sys.version,
   729	        "binaries": {"kissat": KISSAT, "drat_trim_lane_copy": DRAT, "drat_trim_old": DRAT_OLD},
   730	        "inputs": {"encoder": ENC, "skeleton": SKEL, "items": ITEMS, "registry": REGISTRY,
   731	                   "launch_contract": LAUNCH_CONTRACT, "monitor": MONITOR, "observer_source": OBSERVER_SOURCE_SHA},
   732	        "absolute_child_end_epoch": abs_end, "max_chunk_end_epoch": t_start + MAX_CHUNK,
   733	        "retained_output_reservation_bytes": reserve_total, "output_dir": out_dir,
   734	        "limits": {"solver_s": SOLVER_SECONDS, "check_s": CHECK_SECONDS, "grace_s": GRACE, "term_wait_s": TERM_WAIT,
   735	                   "proof_cap_hard": PROOF_CAP, "log_cap_hard": LOG_CAP, "lane_rss_trip": LANE_RSS_TRIP,
   736	                   "free_floor": FREE_FLOOR, "fresh_base": FRESH_BASE},
   737	        "exit_codes": {"0": "plan complete (each item terminal UNSAT/UNKNOWN); not a science verdict",
   738	                       "10": "SAT candidate", "20": "controls failed", "30": "admission refused",
   739	                       "40": "owner cancel", "50": "safety stop", "60": "exception", "70": "prestart refused"},
   740	    }
   741	    write_x(os.path.join(out_dir, "launch-record.json"), launch)
   742	    print("LAUNCH_RECORD", os.path.join(out_dir, "launch-record.json"), flush=True)
   743	    summary = {"schema": "PA19-96H-PILOT-SUMMARY-v5", "items": {}, "controls_ok": None, "stopped": None,
   744	               "terminal_policy": "exit code reports runner execution only; per-item records carry the scientific "
   745	                                  "status; an item counts only if status UNSAT passed qualify_unsat AND the terminal "
   746	                                  "gate; case closure is decided by the parent audit, not by this runner"}
   747	    code = EXIT_EXCEPTION
   748	
   749	    def gate(projected, remaining_reserve):
   750	        why = registry_refusals()
   751	        fb = free_bytes()
   752	        if fb < FRESH_BASE + projected:
   753	            why.append(f"fresh free {fb} < 12.2e9 + {projected}")
   754	        if fb - remaining_reserve < FREE_FLOOR:
   755	            why.append(f"free {fb} - remaining reservations {remaining_reserve} < 12e9")
   756	        if time.time() + ITEM_WORST_SECONDS > min(abs_end, OWNED_SETTLED_BY - 180):
   757	            why.append("chunk-fit/absolute deadline")
   758	        if CANCEL.is_set():
   759	            why.append("owner cancel")
   760	        why += stability_refusals(projected)
   761	        return why, fb
   762	
   763	    try:
   764	        if sha_file(DRAT_OLD[0])[0] != DRAT_OLD[1] or sha_file(KISSAT[0])[0] != KISSAT[1]:
   765	            raise RuntimeError("binary pin mismatch")
   766	        if sha_file(DRAT)[0] != DRAT_OLD[1] or os.lstat(DRAT).st_ino == os.lstat(DRAT_OLD[0]).st_ino:
   767	            raise RuntimeError("vendored drat-trim mismatch or shared inode")
   768	        why, fb = gate(CONTROL_RESERVE, reserve_total)
   769	        if why:
   770	            summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "controls_gate", "why": why, "free": fb}
   771	            code = EXIT_ADMISSION_REFUSED
   772	            return code
   773	        ok, recs = controls(out_dir)
   774	        summary["controls_ok"] = ok
   775	        summary["controls_record_sha256"] = write_x(os.path.join(out_dir, "controls.json"), recs)
   776	        if CANCEL.is_set():
   777	            summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "controls", "why": ["owner cancel"]}
   778	            code = EXIT_CANCELLED
   779	            return code
   780	        if not ok:
   781	            summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "controls", "why": ["a control did not behave as intended"]}
   782	            code = EXIT_CONTROLS_FAILED
   783	            return code
   784	        read_pinned(ENC[0], ENC[1])
   785	        sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
   786	        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("encode_skel_b_lane", ENC[0])
   787	        E = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
   788	        spec.loader.exec_module(E)
   789	        D = json.loads(read_pinned(*SKEL))
   790	        special = [p for p in D["profiles"] if list(p) != P0]
   791	        remaining = len(ITEMS) * ITEM_RESERVE
   792	        for it in ITEMS:
   793	            label = it["id"].replace(":", "_")
   794	            rec = {"id": it["id"], "status": "UNKNOWN", "count_for_scientific_coverage": False,
   795	                   "host_clock_start": clock()}
   796	            why, fb = gate(ITEM_RESERVE, remaining)
   797	            rec["gate"] = {"why": why, "free": fb}
   798	            if why:
   799	                rec["reason"] = "admission refused"
   800	                write_x(os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".record.json"), rec)
   801	                summary["items"][it["id"]] = {"status": "UNKNOWN", "reason": "admission refused"}
   802	                summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "item_gate", "item": it["id"], "why": why}
   803	                code = EXIT_CANCELLED if CANCEL.is_set() else EXIT_ADMISSION_REFUSED
   804	                return code
   805	            Ej = json.loads(read_pinned(*it["list"]))
   806	            if not (Ej["num_placements"] == len(Ej["placements"]) == it["denominator"]):
   807	                raise RuntimeError("list denominator mismatch " + it["id"])
   808	            sk = D["skeletons"][it["b"]]
   809	            nv, cl = E.build_partbatch(D["c0"], [tuple(t) for t in sk["types"]], sk["empty"], special,
   810	                                       [Ej["placements"][i] for i in range(it["lo"], it["hi"] + 1)])
   811	            cnf = E.cnf_bytes(nv, cl)
   812	            del cl
   813	            got = (hashlib.sha256(cnf).hexdigest(), len(cnf), cnf.split(b"\n", 1)[0].decode())
   814	            if got != it["cnf"]:
   815	                raise RuntimeError("CNF digest mismatch %r" % (got,))
   816	            cnf_path = os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".cnf")
   817	            write_bytes_x(cnf_path, cnf)
   818	            del cnf
   819	            if sha_file(cnf_path) != (it["cnf"][0], it["cnf"][1]):
   820	                raise RuntimeError("CNF file re-hash mismatch")
   821	            proof_path = os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".drat")
   822	            ks, kl = run_kissat(cnf_path, proof_path, out_dir, label, SOLVER_SECONDS)
   823	            rec["solver"] = ks
   824	            rec["solver_status_lines"] = [s for s in kl if s.startswith("s ")]
   825	            safety = bool(ks["quarantine_record"]) or ks["group_empty_after_reap"] is not True \
   826	                or (ks["proof_sink"] or {}).get("thread_alive_after_join", False)
   827	            if ks["returncode"] == 10 and rec["solver_status_lines"] == ["s SATISFIABLE"] and ks["stop"] == "self":
   828	                rec["status"] = "SAT_CANDIDATE"
   829	                rec["sat"] = verify_sat(cnf_path, kl, out_dir, label)
   830	                write_x(os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".record.json"), rec)
   831	                summary["items"][it["id"]] = {"status": "SAT_CANDIDATE", "sat": rec["sat"]}
   832	                summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "sat_candidate", "item": it["id"]}
   833	                code = EXIT_SAT_CANDIDATE
   834	                return code
   835	            sink_ok = sink_complete(ks["proof_sink"])
   836	            if sink_ok:
   837	                rec["proof"] = {"sha256": ks["proof_sink"]["sha256_of_written"], "bytes": ks["proof_sink"]["written"]}
   838	            if ks["returncode"] == 20 and ks["stop"] == "self" and ks["trip"] is None and not safety \
   839	                    and sink_ok and not CANCEL.is_set():
   840	                dr, dl = run_drat(cnf_path, proof_path, out_dir, label, CHECK_SECONDS)
   841	                rec["checker"] = dr
   842	                rec["checker_status_lines"] = [s for s in dl if s.startswith("s ")]
   843	                rec["checker_parse_lines"] = [s for s in dl if s.startswith("c parsing") or s.startswith("c finished parsing")]
   844	                post_cnf, post_proof = sha_file(cnf_path), sha_file(proof_path)
   845	                rec["post_check_hashes"] = {"cnf": list(post_cnf), "proof": list(post_proof)}
   846	                rec["defects"] = qualify_unsat(it["cnf"], ks, kl, rec["proof"]["sha256"], rec["proof"]["bytes"],
   847	                                               dr, dl, post_cnf, post_proof)
   848	                safety = safety or bool(dr["quarantine_record"]) or dr["group_empty_after_reap"] is not True
   849	                rec["terminal_gate"] = terminal_gate()
   850	                if not rec["defects"] and not rec["terminal_gate"] and not safety:
   851	                    rec["status"] = "UNSAT"
   852	                    rec["count_for_scientific_coverage"] = True
   853	            else:
   854	                rec["reason"] = "no self-exit rc20 verdict with a complete proof sink (or trip/cancel/safety)"
   855	            rec["host_clock_end"] = clock()
   856	            write_x(os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".record.json"), rec)
   857	            summary["items"][it["id"]] = {k: rec.get(k) for k in ("status", "count_for_scientific_coverage",
   858	                                                                  "defects", "terminal_gate", "reason", "proof")}
   859	            remaining -= ITEM_RESERVE
   860	            if safety:
   861	                summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "safety", "item": it["id"]}
   862	                code = EXIT_SAFETY_STOP
   863	                return code
   864	            if CANCEL.is_set():
   865	                summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "owner_cancel", "item": it["id"]}
   866	                code = EXIT_CANCELLED
   867	                return code
   868	        code = EXIT_PLAN_COMPLETE
   869	        return code
   870	    except BaseException as exc:
   871	        summary["stopped"] = {"phase": "exception", "error": repr(exc)}
   872	        code = EXIT_EXCEPTION
   873	        return code
   874	    finally:
   875	        summary["exit_code"] = code
   876	        summary["host_clock_end"] = clock()
   877	        summary["settlement"] = "every child is reaped in Child.settle before control returns; per-child " \
   878	                                "group_empty_after_reap/quarantine and proof-sink fields are in records; no child " \
   879	                                "is started after an exception"
   880	        write_x(os.path.join(out_dir, "summary.json"), summary)
   881	        print("SUMMARY", os.path.join(out_dir, "summary.json"), "exit", code, flush=True)

   277	
   278	class ProofSink:
   279	    """Owned FIFO reader writing the proof FILE with a HARD byte cap (P4)."""
   280	
   281	    def __init__(self, fifo_path, file_path, cap):
   282	        if os.path.lexists(fifo_path) or os.path.lexists(file_path):
   283	            raise RuntimeError("proof fifo/file path exists")
   284	        os.mkfifo(fifo_path, 0o600)
   285	        self.fifo_path, self.file_path, self.cap = fifo_path, file_path, cap
   286	        self.file = open(file_path, "xb")
   287	        self.sha = hashlib.sha256()
   288	        self.written = self.received = self.discarded = 0
   289	        self.over_cap = self.connected = self.eof = False
   290	        self.error = None
   291	        self.unblocked_by_runner = False
   292	        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
   293	        self.thread.start()
   294	
   295	    def _run(self):
   296	        try:
   297	            fd = os.open(self.fifo_path, os.O_RDONLY)  # blocks until a writer opens the FIFO
   298	            self.connected = True
   299	            try:
   300	                while True:
   301	                    b = os.read(fd, 1 << 20)
   302	                    if not b:
   303	                        self.eof = True
   304	                        break
   305	                    self.received += len(b)
   306	                    if not self.over_cap and self.written + len(b) <= self.cap:
   307	                        self.file.write(b)
   308	                        self.sha.update(b)
   309	                        self.written += len(b)
   310	                    else:
   311	                        self.over_cap = True       # never write beyond the cap; drain so the producer can exit
   312	                        self.discarded += len(b)
   313	            finally:
   314	                os.close(fd)
   315	        except Exception as exc:
   316	            self.error = repr(exc)
   317	        finally:
   318	            try:
   319	                self.file.flush()
   320	                os.fsync(self.file.fileno())
   321	                self.file.close()
   322	            except Exception as exc:
   323	                self.error = (self.error or "") + " close:" + repr(exc)
   324	
   325	    def finish(self):
   326	        """After the producer is reaped: unblock a never-connected reader, then bounded join."""
   327	        deadline = time.time() + 10
   328	        while self.thread.is_alive() and not self.connected and time.time() < deadline:
   329	            try:
   330	                fd = os.open(self.fifo_path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_NONBLOCK)
   331	                self.unblocked_by_runner = True
   332	                os.close(fd)
   333	                break
   334	            except OSError as exc:
   335	                if exc.errno != errno.ENXIO:
   336	                    self.error = (self.error or "") + " unblock:" + repr(exc)
   337	                    break
   338	                time.sleep(0.05)
   339	        self.thread.join(SINK_JOIN_SECONDS)
   340	        rec = {"fifo": self.fifo_path, "file": self.file_path, "cap": self.cap, "connected": self.connected,
   341	               "eof": self.eof, "received": self.received, "written": self.written, "discarded": self.discarded,
   342	               "over_cap": self.over_cap, "error": self.error, "unblocked_by_runner": self.unblocked_by_runner,
   343	               "thread_alive_after_join": self.thread.is_alive(), "sha256_of_written": self.sha.hexdigest()}
   344	        if not rec["thread_alive_after_join"]:
   345	            rec["file_rehash"] = list(sha_file(self.file_path))
   346	        return rec
   347

   349	class Child:
   350	    """One owned native child in its own session; HARD byte-capped stdout+stderr capture file."""
   351	
   352	    def __init__(self, argv, out_path, cap):
   353	        self.argv, self.out_path, self.cap = argv, out_path, cap
   354	        self.captured, self.over_cap, self.reader_error = 0, False, None
   355	        self.term_sent_at, self.stop = None, None
   356	        self.sink = open(out_path, "xb")  # exclusive capture file BEFORE the child exists
   357	        try:
   358	            self.proc = subprocess.Popen(argv, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
   359	                                         stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True,
   360	                                         close_fds=True, cwd=LANE + "/tmp", env=CHILD_ENV)
   361	        except BaseException:
   362	            self.sink.close()
   363	            raise
   364	        self.pid = self.pgid = self.proc.pid
   365	        self.started = time.time()
   366	        try:
   367	            self.identity_at_start = identity(self.pid)
   368	            self.reader = threading.Thread(target=self._read, daemon=True)
   369	            self.reader.start()
   370	        except BaseException:
   371	            # never leave an owned child unsupervised: one SIGTERM to the unreaped child, then wait
   372	            self.proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)
   373	            self.proc.wait()
   374	            self.sink.close()
   375	            raise
   376	
   377	    def _read(self):
   378	        try:
   379	            while True:
   380	                b = self.proc.stdout.read1(65536)
   381	                if not b:
   382	                    break
   383	                room = self.cap - self.captured
   384	                if room > 0:
   385	                    self.sink.write(b[:room])
   386	                self.captured += len(b)
   387	                if self.captured > self.cap:
   388	                    self.over_cap = True
   389	        except Exception as exc:
   390	            self.reader_error = repr(exc)
   391	        finally:
   392	            try:
   393	                self.sink.flush()
   394	                os.fsync(self.sink.fileno())
   395	                self.sink.close()
   396	                self.proc.stdout.close()
   397	            except Exception as exc:
   398	                self.reader_error = (self.reader_error or "") + " close:" + repr(exc)
   399	
   400	    def alive(self):
   401	        return self.proc.poll() is None
   402	
   403	    def term(self, reason):
   404	        if self.term_sent_at is None and self.proc.returncode is None:
   405	            self.stop = reason
   406	            self.term_sent_at = time.time()
   407	            self.proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)  # Popen polls first; never signals a reaped PID
   408	
   409	    def settle(self, out_dir, label):
   410	        """Explicit wait; after the single SIGTERM no further signal is ever sent."""
   411	        quarantine = None
   412	        if self.term_sent_at is not None:
   413	            deadline = self.term_sent_at + TERM_WAIT
   414	            while self.proc.poll() is None and time.time() < deadline:
   415	                time.sleep(0.1)
   416	            if self.proc.poll() is None:
   417	                quarantine = os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".QUARANTINE.json")
   418	                write_x(quarantine, {"host_clock": clock(), "pid": self.pid, "pgid": self.pgid,
   419	                                     "argv": self.argv, "note": "survived SIGTERM + 60 s; no further signal; "
   420	                                     "runner keeps waiting and starts no new child"})
   421	        rc = self.proc.wait()
   422	        self.reader.join()
   423	        try:
   424	            os.killpg(self.pgid, 0)
   425	            group_empty = False
   426	        except ProcessLookupError:
   427	            group_empty = True
   428	        except PermissionError:
   429	            group_empty = False
   430	        return {"pid": self.pid, "pgid": self.pgid, "identity_at_start": self.identity_at_start,
   431	                "argv": self.argv, "returncode": rc, "stop": self.stop or "self",
   432	                "seconds": round(time.time() - self.started, 3), "captured_bytes": self.captured,
   433	                "capture_over_cap": self.over_cap, "reader_error": self.reader_error,
   434	                "group_empty_after_reap": group_empty, "quarantine_record": quarantine,
   435	                "output_path": self.out_path, "output_sha256": sha_file(self.out_path)[0]}
   436

   438	def segments(path):
   439	    with open(path, "rb") as f:
   440	        txt = f.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
   441	    return [s for s in re.split(r"[\r\n]", txt) if s != ""]

   127	def clock():
   128	    return subprocess.run(["/bin/date", "+%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%z"], capture_output=True, text=True,
   129	                          check=True, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL).stdout.strip()

   131	
   132	def sha_file(path):
   133	    st = os.lstat(path)
   134	    if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode):
   135	        raise RuntimeError("not a regular file: " + path)
   136	    h = hashlib.sha256()
   137	    n = 0
   138	    with open(path, "rb") as f:
   139	        for b in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
   140	            h.update(b)
   141	            n += len(b)
   142	    return h.hexdigest(), n
   143	
   144	
   145	def read_pinned(path, expect):
   146	    st = os.lstat(path)
   147	    if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode):
   148	        raise RuntimeError("not a regular file: " + path)
   149	    with open(path, "rb") as f:
   150	        raw = f.read()
   151	    got = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
   152	    if got != expect:
   153	        raise RuntimeError(f"pin mismatch {path}: {got} != {expect}")
   154	    return raw
   155

   157	def write_x(path, obj):
   158	    data = json.dumps(obj, indent=1, sort_keys=True).encode()
   159	    with open(path, "xb") as f:
   160	        f.write(data)
   161	        f.flush()
   162	        os.fsync(f.fileno())
   163	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
   164	
   165	
   166	def write_bytes_x(path, data):
   167	    with open(path, "xb") as f:
   168	        f.write(data)
   169	        f.flush()
   170	        os.fsync(f.fileno())
   171	    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), len(data)

   174	def free_bytes():
   175	    st = os.statvfs(LANE)
   176	    return st.f_bavail * st.f_frsize

   179	def rss_bytes(pids):
   180	    out = subprocess.run(["/bin/ps", "-o", "rss=", "-p", ",".join(str(p) for p in pids)],
   181	                         capture_output=True, text=True, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL)
   182	    vals = [int(x) for x in out.stdout.split() if x.strip().isdigit()]
   183	    return sum(vals) * 1024, len(vals)

   186	def identity(pid):
   187	    try:
   188	        out = subprocess.run(["/bin/ps", "-o", "pid=,ppid=,pgid=,lstart=,comm=", "-p", str(pid)],
   189	                             capture_output=True, text=True, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL)
   190	        return out.stdout.strip()
   191	    except Exception as exc:  # identity is evidence only; never let it orphan a child
   192	        return "unavailable: %r" % exc

   195	def registry_refusals():
   196	    """Fail-closed STOP registry: new-round STOP*/HALT*/SAT-CANDIDATE* markers in LANE/control or
   197	    RUN/control; any change, loss or addition in the inherited old cycle2-root STOP/HALT set."""
   198	    why = []
   199	    for d in (LANE + "/control", RUN + "/control"):
   200	        for name in sorted(os.listdir(d)):
   201	            if re.match(r"(?i)^(stop|halt|sat-candidate)", name) or "HALT" in name:
   202	                why.append("new-round marker " + os.path.join(d, name))
   203	    c2 = OLD + "/cycle2"
   204	    present = sorted(n for n in os.listdir(c2) if re.match(r"^(STOP|HALT)", n) or "HALT" in n)
   205	    if present != sorted(OLD_LIVE_MARKERS):
   206	        why.append("inherited cycle2 marker set changed: " + json.dumps(present))
   207	    for name, h in OLD_LIVE_MARKERS.items():
   208	        try:
   209	            if sha_file(os.path.join(c2, name))[0] != h:
   210	                why.append("inherited marker changed: " + name)
   211	        except Exception as exc:
   212	            why.append(f"inherited marker unreadable: {name}: {exc!r}")
   213	    try:
   214	        if sha_file(REGISTRY[0])[0] != REGISTRY[1]:
   215	            why.append("lane registry changed")
   216	        if sha_file(LAUNCH_CONTRACT[0])[0] != LAUNCH_CONTRACT[1]:
   217	            why.append("launch contract changed")
   218	    except Exception as exc:
   219	        why.append("registry/contract unreadable: %r" % exc)
   220	    return why

   278	class ProofSink:
   279	    """Owned FIFO reader writing the proof FILE with a HARD byte cap (P4)."""
   280	
   281	    def __init__(self, fifo_path, file_path, cap):
   282	        if os.path.lexists(fifo_path) or os.path.lexists(file_path):
   283	            raise RuntimeError("proof fifo/file path exists")
   284	        os.mkfifo(fifo_path, 0o600)
   285	        self.fifo_path, self.file_path, self.cap = fifo_path, file_path, cap
   286	        self.file = open(file_path, "xb")
   287	        self.sha = hashlib.sha256()
   288	        self.written = self.received = self.discarded = 0
   289	        self.over_cap = self.connected = self.eof = False
   290	        self.error = None
   291	        self.unblocked_by_runner = False
   292	        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
   293	        self.thread.start()
   294	
   295	    def _run(self):
   296	        try:
   297	            fd = os.open(self.fifo_path, os.O_RDONLY)  # blocks until a writer opens the FIFO
   298	            self.connected = True
   299	            try:
   300	                while True:
   301	                    b = os.read(fd, 1 << 20)
   302	                    if not b:
   303	                        self.eof = True
   304	                        break
   305	                    self.received += len(b)
   306	                    if not self.over_cap and self.written + len(b) <= self.cap:
   307	                        self.file.write(b)
   308	                        self.sha.update(b)
   309	                        self.written += len(b)
   310	                    else:
   311	                        self.over_cap = True       # never write beyond the cap; drain so the producer can exit
   312	                        self.discarded += len(b)
   313	            finally:
   314	                os.close(fd)
   315	        except Exception as exc:
   316	            self.error = repr(exc)
   317	        finally:
   318	            try:
   319	                self.file.flush()
   320	                os.fsync(self.file.fileno())
   321	                self.file.close()
   322	            except Exception as exc:
   323	                self.error = (self.error or "") + " close:" + repr(exc)
   324	
   325	    def finish(self):
   326	        """After the producer is reaped: unblock a never-connected reader, then bounded join."""
   327	        deadline = time.time() + 10
   328	        while self.thread.is_alive() and not self.connected and time.time() < deadline:
   329	            try:
   330	                fd = os.open(self.fifo_path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_NONBLOCK)
   331	                self.unblocked_by_runner = True
   332	                os.close(fd)
   333	                break
   334	            except OSError as exc:
   335	                if exc.errno != errno.ENXIO:
   336	                    self.error = (self.error or "") + " unblock:" + repr(exc)
   337	                    break
   338	                time.sleep(0.05)
   339	        self.thread.join(SINK_JOIN_SECONDS)
   340	        rec = {"fifo": self.fifo_path, "file": self.file_path, "cap": self.cap, "connected": self.connected,
   341	               "eof": self.eof, "received": self.received, "written": self.written, "discarded": self.discarded,
   342	               "over_cap": self.over_cap, "error": self.error, "unblocked_by_runner": self.unblocked_by_runner,
   343	               "thread_alive_after_join": self.thread.is_alive(), "sha256_of_written": self.sha.hexdigest()}
   344	        if not rec["thread_alive_after_join"]:
   345	            rec["file_rehash"] = list(sha_file(self.file_path))
   346	        return rec
   347	
   348	
   349	class Child:
   350	    """One owned native child in its own session; HARD byte-capped stdout+stderr capture file."""
   351	
   352	    def __init__(self, argv, out_path, cap):
   353	        self.argv, self.out_path, self.cap = argv, out_path, cap
   354	        self.captured, self.over_cap, self.reader_error = 0, False, None
   355	        self.term_sent_at, self.stop = None, None
   356	        self.sink = open(out_path, "xb")  # exclusive capture file BEFORE the child exists
   357	        try:
   358	            self.proc = subprocess.Popen(argv, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
   359	                                         stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True,
   360	                                         close_fds=True, cwd=LANE + "/tmp", env=CHILD_ENV)
   361	        except BaseException:
   362	            self.sink.close()
   363	            raise
   364	        self.pid = self.pgid = self.proc.pid
   365	        self.started = time.time()
   366	        try:
   367	            self.identity_at_start = identity(self.pid)
   368	            self.reader = threading.Thread(target=self._read, daemon=True)
   369	            self.reader.start()
   370	        except BaseException:
   371	            # never leave an owned child unsupervised: one SIGTERM to the unreaped child, then wait
   372	            self.proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)
   373	            self.proc.wait()
   374	            self.sink.close()
   375	            raise
   376	
   377	    def _read(self):
   378	        try:
   379	            while True:
   380	                b = self.proc.stdout.read1(65536)
   381	                if not b:
   382	                    break
   383	                room = self.cap - self.captured
   384	                if room > 0:
   385	                    self.sink.write(b[:room])
   386	                self.captured += len(b)
   387	                if self.captured > self.cap:
   388	                    self.over_cap = True
   389	        except Exception as exc:
   390	            self.reader_error = repr(exc)
   391	        finally:
   392	            try:
   393	                self.sink.flush()
   394	                os.fsync(self.sink.fileno())
   395	                self.sink.close()
   396	                self.proc.stdout.close()
   397	            except Exception as exc:
   398	                self.reader_error = (self.reader_error or "") + " close:" + repr(exc)
   399	
   400	    def alive(self):
   401	        return self.proc.poll() is None
   402	
   403	    def term(self, reason):
   404	        if self.term_sent_at is None and self.proc.returncode is None:
   405	            self.stop = reason
   406	            self.term_sent_at = time.time()
   407	            self.proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)  # Popen polls first; never signals a reaped PID
   408	
   409	    def settle(self, out_dir, label):
   410	        """Explicit wait; after the single SIGTERM no further signal is ever sent."""
   411	        quarantine = None
   412	        if self.term_sent_at is not None:
   413	            deadline = self.term_sent_at + TERM_WAIT
   414	            while self.proc.poll() is None and time.time() < deadline:
   415	                time.sleep(0.1)
   416	            if self.proc.poll() is None:
   417	                quarantine = os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".QUARANTINE.json")
   418	                write_x(quarantine, {"host_clock": clock(), "pid": self.pid, "pgid": self.pgid,
   419	                                     "argv": self.argv, "note": "survived SIGTERM + 60 s; no further signal; "
   420	                                     "runner keeps waiting and starts no new child"})
   421	        rc = self.proc.wait()
   422	        self.reader.join()
   423	        try:
   424	            os.killpg(self.pgid, 0)
   425	            group_empty = False
   426	        except ProcessLookupError:
   427	            group_empty = True
   428	        except PermissionError:
   429	            group_empty = False
   430	        return {"pid": self.pid, "pgid": self.pgid, "identity_at_start": self.identity_at_start,
   431	                "argv": self.argv, "returncode": rc, "stop": self.stop or "self",
   432	                "seconds": round(time.time() - self.started, 3), "captured_bytes": self.captured,
   433	                "capture_over_cap": self.over_cap, "reader_error": self.reader_error,
   434	                "group_empty_after_reap": group_empty, "quarantine_record": quarantine,
   435	                "output_path": self.out_path, "output_sha256": sha_file(self.out_path)[0]}
   436

   438	def segments(path):
   439	    with open(path, "rb") as f:
   440	        txt = f.read().decode("utf-8", "replace")
   441	    return [s for s in re.split(r"[\r\n]", txt) if s != ""]

   444	def supervise(child, wall, sink):
   445	    """Monitor until exit or a single trip; any monitor exception is itself a trip."""
   446	    me = os.getpid()
   447	    last_rss = last_reg = 0.0
   448	    peak = 0
   449	    while child.alive():
   450	        try:
   451	            now = time.time()
   452	            if CANCEL.is_set():
   453	                child.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
   454	            elif now - child.started > wall + GRACE:
   455	                child.term("runner_wall_bound")
   456	            elif sink is not None and (sink.over_cap or sink.error):
   457	                child.term("proof_cap" if sink.over_cap else "proof_sink_error")
   458	            elif child.over_cap:
   459	                child.term("capture_cap")
   460	            elif free_bytes() < FREE_FLOOR:
   461	                child.term("inflight_disk_floor")
   462	            if child.term_sent_at is None and now - last_rss >= 1.0:
   463	                last_rss = now
   464	                r, n = rss_bytes([me, child.pid])
   465	                peak = max(peak, r)
   466	                if n < 2 and child.alive():
   467	                    child.term("rss_unreadable")
   468	                elif r > LANE_RSS_TRIP:
   469	                    child.term("lane_rss")
   470	            if child.term_sent_at is None and now - last_reg >= 5.0:
   471	                last_reg = now
   472	                if registry_refusals():
   473	                    child.term("stop_registry")
   474	        except Exception as exc:
   475	            child.term("monitor_exception:%r" % exc)
   476	        if child.term_sent_at is not None:
   477	            break
   478	        time.sleep(0.25)
   479	    return child.stop, peak
   480	
   481	
   482	def run_child(argv, out_dir, label, wall, sink=None):
   483	    ch = Child(argv, os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".out"), LOG_CAP)
   484	    try:
   485	        if CANCEL.is_set():
   486	            ch.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
   487	        trip, peak = supervise(ch, wall, sink)
   488	    except BaseException as exc:
   489	        ch.term("runner_exception:%r" % exc)
   490	        trip, peak = ch.stop, None

   498	def run_kissat(cnf_path, proof_file, out_dir, label, seconds, proof_cap=PROOF_CAP):
   499	    """kissat with its proof routed through a hard-capped owned sink; proof_file None = no proof."""

   519	def sink_complete(sink_rec):
   520	    return (sink_rec is not None and sink_rec["connected"] and sink_rec["eof"] and not sink_rec["over_cap"]
   521	            and sink_rec["error"] is None and not sink_rec["unblocked_by_runner"]
   522	            and not sink_rec["thread_alive_after_join"] and sink_rec["discarded"] == 0
   523	            and sink_rec["received"] == sink_rec["written"]
   524	            and sink_rec.get("file_rehash") == [sink_rec["sha256_of_written"], sink_rec["written"]])

   527	def qualify_unsat(cnf_expect, solver_rec, kissat_lines, proof_sha, proof_bytes, drat_rec, drat_lines,
   528	                  cnf_post, proof_post):
   529	    bad = []
   530	    if solver_rec["returncode"] != 20 or solver_rec["stop"] != "self" or solver_rec["trip"] is not None:
   531	        bad.append("solver not self-exit rc20")
   532	    if [s for s in kissat_lines if s.startswith("s ")] != ["s UNSATISFIABLE"]:
   533	        bad.append("solver status lines not exactly ['s UNSATISFIABLE']")
   534	    if solver_rec.get("proof_sink") is not None and not sink_complete(solver_rec["proof_sink"]):
   535	        bad.append("proof sink incomplete/over cap/error")
   536	    if proof_bytes <= 0 or proof_bytes > PROOF_CAP:
   537	        bad.append("proof size outside (0, cap]")
   538	    if drat_rec["returncode"] != 0 or drat_rec["stop"] != "self" or drat_rec["trip"] is not None:
   539	        bad.append("checker not self-exit rc0")
   540	    st = [s for s in drat_lines if s.startswith("s ")]
   541	    if st != ["s VERIFIED"]:
   542	        bad.append("checker status lines %r" % st)
   543	    v, c = cnf_expect[2].split()[2:4]
   544	    if ("c parsing input formula with %s variables and %s clauses" % (v, c)) not in drat_lines:
   545	        bad.append("checker parse header line missing/mismatched")
   546	    if ("c finished parsing, read %d bytes from proof file" % proof_bytes) not in drat_lines:
   547	        bad.append("checker-consumed proof bytes != proof file bytes")
   548	    for pat in ("early EOF", "clauses less than expected", "trivial UNSAT", "ERROR", "MEMOUT",
   549	                "wrong binary prefix", "no empty clause detected", "NOT VERIFIED", "TIMEOUT"):
   550	        if any(pat in s for s in drat_lines):
   551	            bad.append("checker output contains %r" % pat)
   552	    if cnf_post != (cnf_expect[0], cnf_expect[1]):
   553	        bad.append("CNF file changed during run")
   554	    if proof_post != (proof_sha, proof_bytes):
   555	        bad.append("proof file changed during check")
   556	    for rec in (solver_rec, drat_rec):
   557	        if rec["group_empty_after_reap"] is not True or rec["quarantine_record"] or rec["reader_error"] \
   558	                or rec["capture_over_cap"]:
   559	            bad.append("settlement/capture defect pid %s" % rec["pid"])
   560	    return bad

   563	def terminal_gate():
   564	    """P2: final consumer gate evaluated immediately before a counted status is written."""
   565	    why = []
   566	    if CANCEL.is_set():
   567	        why.append("cancel latched")
   568	    try:
   569	        why += registry_refusals()
   570	        if free_bytes() < FREE_FLOOR:
   571	            why.append("free below in-flight floor at terminal gate")
   572	    except Exception as exc:
   573	        why.append("terminal gate exception %r" % exc)
   574	    return why

   577	def verify_sat(cnf_path, kissat_lines, out_dir, label):
   578	    """Two independent checks of a SAT model: clause satisfaction and both PA19 definitions."""
   579	    lits = set()
   580	    for s in kissat_lines:
   581	        if s.startswith("v "):
   582	            lits.update(int(x) for x in s[2:].split() if x != "0")
   583	    sat_all = True
   584	    with open(cnf_path, "rb") as f:
   585	        for ln in f:
   586	            if ln.startswith(b"p") or ln.startswith(b"c"):
   587	                continue
   588	            cl = [int(x) for x in ln.split()[:-1]]
   589	            if not any(l in lits for l in cl):
   590	                sat_all = False
   591	                break
   592	    A = [[None] * 8 for _ in range(19)]
   593	    cells_ok = True
   594	    for r in range(19):
   595	        for c in range(8):
   596	            ss = [s for s in range(6) if (1 + 48 * r + 6 * c + s) in lits]
   597	            if len(ss) != 1:
   598	                cells_ok = False
   599	            else:
   600	                A[r][c] = ss[0]
   601	    rowpair_ok = cells_ok and all(sum(A[r][c] == A[q][c] for c in range(8)) <= 1
   602	                                  for r in range(19) for q in range(r + 1, 19))
   603	    colpair_ok = cells_ok and all(len({(A[r][c], A[r][d]) for r in range(19)}) == 19
   604	                                  for c in range(8) for d in range(c + 1, 8))
   605	    cand = os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".SAT-CANDIDATE.json")
   606	    write_x(cand, {"host_clock": clock(), "array": A, "clauses_all_satisfied": sat_all,
   607	                   "cells_exactly_one": cells_ok, "row_pair_agreements_le_1": rowpair_ok,
   608	                   "column_pair_ordered_pairs_distinct": colpair_ok,
   609	                   "status": "CANDIDATE_UNVERIFIED - notify parent immediately"})
   610	    latch = os.path.join(LANE, "control", "SAT-CANDIDATE-" + label)
   611	    write_bytes_x(latch, cand.encode())
   612	    return {"candidate": cand, "latch": latch, "clauses_all_satisfied": sat_all, "cells": cells_ok,
   613	            "rowpair": rowpair_ok, "colpair": colpair_ok}

   616	def php_cnf(p, h):
   617	    """Pigeonhole: p pigeons into h holes; UNSAT iff p > h."""
   618	    var = lambda i, j: i * h + j + 1  # noqa: E731
   619	    cl = [[var(i, j) for j in range(h)] for i in range(p)]
   620	    for j in range(h):
   621	        for i in range(p):
   622	            for k in range(i + 1, p):
   623	                cl.append([-var(i, j), -var(k, j)])
   624	    return ("p cnf %d %d\n" % (p * h, len(cl)) + "".join(" ".join(map(str, c)) + " 0\n" for c in cl)).encode()

   964	long matchClause (struct solver* S, long *clauselist, int listsize, int* input, int size) {
   965	  int i, j;
   966	  for (i = 0; i < listsize; ++i) {
   967	    int *clause = S->DB + clauselist[i];

   977	unsigned int getHash (int* input) {
   978	  unsigned int sum = 0, prod = 1, xor = 0;
   979	  while (*input) {
   980	    prod *= *input; sum += *input; xor ^= *input; input++; }
   981	  return (1023 * sum + prod ^ (31 * xor)) % BIGINIT; }
   982	
   983	int read_lit (struct solver *S, int *lit) {
   984	  int l = 0, lc, shift = 0;
   985	  do {
   986	    lc = getc_unlocked (S->proofFile);
   987	    S->nReads++;
   988	    if ((shift == 0) && (lc == EOF)) return EOF;
   989	    l |= (lc & 127) << shift;
   990	    shift += 7; }
   991	  while (lc > 127);
   992	  if (l % 2) *lit = (l >> 1) * -1;
   993	  else       *lit = (l >> 1);
   994	  return 1; }
   995	
   996	void deactivate (struct solver *S) {
   997	  S->nActive = 0;
   998	  int step;
   999	  for (step = 0; step < S->nStep; step++) {
  1000	    if ((S->proof[step] & 1) == 0) {
  1001	      int *clause = S->DB + (S->proof[step] >> INFOBITS);
  1002	      if (clause[ID] & ACTIVE) clause[ID] ^= ACTIVE; } }
  1003	}
  1004	
  1005	void shuffleProof (struct solver *S, int iteration) {
  1006	  int i, step, _step;
  1007	
  1008	  double base = 100;
  1009	  for (i = 1; i < iteration; i++)
  1010	    base *= 1.1;
  1011	
  1012	  // randomly remove clause deletion steps
  1013	  for (_step = 0, step = 0; step < S->nStep; step++) {
  1014	    if (S->proof[step] & 1) {
  1015	      int length = 0;
  1016	      int *clause = S->DB + (S->proof[step] >> INFOBITS);
  1017	      while (*clause) { length++; clause++; }
  1018	      if ((rand() % 1000) < (base * iteration / length)) continue; }
  1019	    S->proof[_step++] = S->proof[step]; }
  1020	  S->nStep = _step;
  1021	
  1022	  for (step = S->nStep - 1; step > 0; step--) {
  1023	    long a = S->proof[step  ];
  1024	    if (a & DBIT) continue;
  1025	    long b = S->proof[step-1];
  1026	    if (b & DBIT) {
  1027	      S->proof[step  ] = b;
  1028	      S->proof[step-1] = a; }
  1029	    else {
  1030	      int *c = S->DB + (a >> INFOBITS);
  1031	      int *d = S->DB + (b >> INFOBITS);
  1032	      int coinflip = 0;
  1033	//      int coinflip = rand () / (RAND_MAX >> 1);
  1034	      if (c[MAXDEP] < d[MAXDEP] || (coinflip && (c[MAXDEP] < d[ID]))) {
  1035	        int tmp = d[ID];
  1036	        d[ID] = c[ID];
  1037	        c[ID] = tmp;
  1038	        S->proof[step  ] = b;
  1039	        S->proof[step-1] = a; } } }
  1040	
  1041	  for (step = 0; step < S->nStep; step++) {
  1042	    long ad = S->proof[step];
  1043	    if (ad & 1) continue;
  1044	    int *clause = S->DB + (ad >> INFOBITS);
  1045	    int i, length = 0;
  1046	    while (*clause) { length++; clause++; }
  1047	    clause = S->DB + (ad >> INFOBITS);
  1048	    for (i = 0; i < length - 1; i++) {
  1049	      int j = i + rand() / (RAND_MAX / (length - i) + 1);
  1050	      int t = clause[i]; clause[i] = clause[j]; clause[j] = t; } } }

  1052	int parse (struct solver* S) {

  1360	int binChar (int c) {
  1361	  // ASCII characters: 10 (LF), 13 (CR), 32 (' '), 45 ('-'), 48 ('0'), 57 ('9'), 99 ('c'), 100 ('d')
  1362	  if ((c != 10) && (c != 13) && (c != 32) && (c != 45) && ((c < 48) || (c > 57)) && (c != 99) && (c != 100))

   178	+class ProofSink:
   179	+    """Owned FIFO reader writing the proof FILE with a HARD byte cap (P4)."""
   180	+
   181	+    def __init__(self, fifo_path, file_path, cap):
   182	+        if os.path.lexists(fifo_path) or os.path.lexists(file_path):
   183	+            raise RuntimeError("proof fifo/file path exists")
   184	+        os.mkfifo(fifo_path, 0o600)
   185	+        self.fifo_path, self.file_path, self.cap = fifo_path, file_path, cap
   186	+        self.file = open(file_path, "xb")
   187	+        self.sha = hashlib.sha256()
   188	+        self.written = self.received = self.discarded = 0
   189	+        self.over_cap = self.connected = self.eof = False
   190	+        self.error = None
   191	+        self.unblocked_by_runner = False
   192	+        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
   193	+        self.thread.start()
   194	+
   195	+    def _run(self):
   196	+        try:
   197	+            fd = os.open(self.fifo_path, os.O_RDONLY)  # blocks until a writer opens the FIFO
   198	+            self.connected = True
   199	+            try:
   200	+                while True:
   201	+                    b = os.read(fd, 1 << 20)
   202	+                    if not b:
   203	+                        self.eof = True
   204	+                        break
   205	+                    self.received += len(b)
   206	+                    if not self.over_cap and self.written + len(b) <= self.cap:
   207	+                        self.file.write(b)
   208	+                        self.sha.update(b)
   209	+                        self.written += len(b)
   210	+                    else:
   211	+                        self.over_cap = True       # never write beyond the cap; drain so the producer can exit
   212	+                        self.discarded += len(b)
   213	+            finally:
   214	+                os.close(fd)
   215	+        except Exception as exc:
   216	+            self.error = repr(exc)
   217	+        finally:
   218	+            try:
   219	+                self.file.flush()
   220	+                os.fsync(self.file.fileno())
   221	+                self.file.close()
   222	+            except Exception as exc:
   223	+                self.error = (self.error or "") + " close:" + repr(exc)
   224	+
   225	+    def finish(self):
   226	+        """After the producer is reaped: unblock a never-connected reader, then bounded join."""
   227	+        deadline = time.time() + 10
   228	+        while self.thread.is_alive() and not self.connected and time.time() < deadline:
   229	+            try:
   230	+                fd = os.open(self.fifo_path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_NONBLOCK)
   231	+                self.unblocked_by_runner = True
   232	+                os.close(fd)
   233	+                break
   234	+            except OSError as exc:
   235	+                if exc.errno != errno.ENXIO:
   236	+                    self.error = (self.error or "") + " unblock:" + repr(exc)
   237	+                    break
   238	+                time.sleep(0.05)
   239	+        self.thread.join(SINK_JOIN_SECONDS)
   240	+        rec = {"fifo": self.fifo_path, "file": self.file_path, "cap": self.cap, "connected": self.connected,

    69	static inline void assign (struct solver* S, int lit) {
    70	  S->falseA[-lit] = 1; *(S->assigned++) = -lit; }

    72	int compare (const void *a, const void *b) {

    82	static inline void addWatchPtr (struct solver* S, int lit, long watch) {
   230	  if (check) goto flip_check;
   231	  S->processed = S->assigned;
   232	  return SAT; }	                                       // Finally, no conflict was found

   235	// Propagate top level units
   236	static inline int propagateUnits (struct solver* S, int init) {
   237	  int i;
   238	//  printf("c propagateUnits %i\n", S->unitSize);
   239	  while (S->forced > S->falseStack) {
   240	    S->falseA[*(--S->forced)] = 0;
   241	    S->reason[abs (*S->forced)] = 0; }
   242	  S->forced = S->assigned = S->processed = S->falseStack;
   243	  for (i = 0; i < S->unitSize; i++) {
   244	    int lit = S->DB[ S->unitStack[i] ];
   245	    S->reason[abs (lit)] = S->unitStack[i] + 1;
   246	    assign (S, lit); }
   247	
   248	  if (propagate (S, init, 1) == UNSAT) { return UNSAT; }
   249	  S->forced = S->processed;
   250	  return SAT; }

   252	// Put falsified literals at the end and returns the size under the current
   253	// assignment: negative size means satisfied, size = 0 means falsified
   254	int sortSize (struct solver *S, int *lemma) {
   255	  unsigned int size = 0, last = 0, sat = 1;
   705	  S->assigned   = S->falseStack; // Points inside *falseStack at last unprocessed literal
   706	
   707	  // initialize watch pointers on the original clauses
   708	  S->RATmode    = 0;
   709	  S->nRemoved   = 0;
   710	  S->nOpt       = 0;
   711	  S->nResolve   = 0;
   712	  S->RATcount   = 0;
   713	  S->nActive    = 0;
   714	  S->COREcount  = 0;
   715	  S->unitSize   = 0;
   716	
   717	  int i;
   718	  for (i = 1; i <= S->maxVar; ++i) {
   719	    S->reason    [i]                   = 0;
   720	    S->falseStack[i]                   = 0;
   721	    S->falseA[i]    = S->falseA[-i]    = 0;
   722	    S->used  [i]    = S->used  [-i]    = 0;
   723	    S->wlist [i][0] = S->wlist [-i][0] = END; }
   724	
   725	  for (i = 0; i < S->nClauses; i++) {
   726	    int *clause = S->DB + (S->formula[i] >> INFOBITS);
   727	    if (clause[ID] & ACTIVE) clause[ID] ^= ACTIVE;
   728	    if (clause[0] == 0) {
   729	      printf ("\rc formula contains empty clause\n");
   730	      if (S->coreStr) {
   731	        FILE *coreFile = fopen (S->coreStr, "w");
   732	        fprintf (coreFile, "p cnf 0 1\n 0\n");
   733	        fclose (coreFile); }
   734	      if (S->lemmaStr) {
   735	        FILE *lemmaFile = fopen (S->lemmaStr, "w");
   736	        fprintf (lemmaFile, "0\n");
   737	        fclose (lemmaFile); }
   738	      return UNSAT; }
   739	    if (clause[1]) { addWatch (S, clause, 0); addWatch (S, clause, 1); }
   740	    else if (S->falseA[clause[0]]) {
   741	      printf ("\rc found complementary unit clauses: %i\n", clause[0]);
   742	      if (S->coreStr) {
   743	        FILE *coreFile = fopen (S->coreStr, "w");
   744	        fprintf (coreFile, "p cnf %i 2\n%i 0\n%i 0\n", abs (clause[0]), clause[0], -clause[0]);
   745	        fclose (coreFile); }
   746	      if (S->lemmaStr) {
   747	        FILE *lemmaFile = fopen (S->lemmaStr, "w");
   748	        fprintf (lemmaFile, "0\n");
   749	        fclose (lemmaFile); }
   750	      if (S->lratFile) {
   751	        int j;
   752	        for (j = 0; j < i; j++) {
   753	          int *_clause = S->DB + (S->formula[j] >> INFOBITS);
   754	          if ((_clause[0] == -clause[0]) && !_clause[1]) break; }
   755	        fprintf (S->lratFile, "%li 0 %i %i 0\n", S->nClauses + 1, j + 1, i + 1); }
   756	      return UNSAT; }
   757	    else if (!S->falseA[ -clause[0] ]) {
   758	      addUnit (S, (long) (clause - S->DB));
   759	      assign (S, clause[0]); } }
   760	
   761	  S->nDependencies = 0;
   762	  S->time = S->count; // Alternative time init
   763	  if (propagateUnits (S, 1) == UNSAT) {
   764	    printf ("\rc UNSAT via unit propagation on the input instance\n");
   765	    printDependencies (S, NULL, 0);
   766	    postprocess (S); return UNSAT; }
   767	  return SAT; }

   769	int verify (struct solver *S, int begin, int end) {
   770	  int top_flag = 1;
   771	  if (init (S) == UNSAT) return UNSAT;
   772	
   773	  if (S->mode == FORWARD_UNSAT) {
   774	    if (begin == end)
   775	      printf ("\rc start forward verification\n"); }
   776	
   777	  int step;
   778	  int adds = 0;
   779	  int active = S->nClauses;
   780	  for (step = 0; step < S->nStep; step++) {
   781	    if (step >= begin && step < end) continue;
   782	    long ad = S->proof[step]; long d = ad & 1;
   783	    int *lemmas = S->DB + (ad >> INFOBITS);
   784	
   785	    S->time = lemmas[ID];
   786	    if (d) { active--; }
   787	    else   { active++; adds++; }
   788	    if (S->mode == FORWARD_SAT && S->verb) printf ("\rc %i active clauses\n", active);
   789	
   790	    if (!lemmas[1]) { // found a unit
   791	      int lit = lemmas[0];
   792	      if (S->verb)
   793	        printf ("\rc found unit in proof %i [%li]\n", lit, S->time);
   794	      if (d) {
   795	        if (S->mode == FORWARD_SAT) {
   796	          removeUnit (S, lit); propagateUnits (S, 0); }
   797	        else { // no need to remove units while checking UNSAT
   798	          if (S->verb) { printf("c removing proof step: d "); printClause(lemmas); }
   799	          S->proof[step] = 0; continue; } }
   800	      else {
   801	//        printf ("c unit %i\n", lit);
   802	//        if (S->falseA[-lit]) S->reason[abs(lit)] = (lemmas - S->DB) + 1;
   803	        if (S->mode == BACKWARD_UNSAT && S->falseA[-lit]) { S->proof[step] = 0; continue; }
   804	        else { addUnit (S, (long) (lemmas - S->DB)); } } }
   805	
   806	    if (d && lemmas[1]) { // if delete and not unit
   807	      if ((S->reason[abs (lemmas[0])] - 1) == (lemmas - S->DB)) { // what is this check?
   808	        if (S->mode != FORWARD_SAT) { // ignore pseudo unit clause deletion
   809	          if (S->warning != NOWARNING) {
   810	            if (S->verb) { printf ("\rc WARNING: ignoring deletion instruction %li: ", (lemmas - S->DB)); printClause (lemmas); } }
   811	          if (S->warning == HARDWARNING) exit (HARDWARNING);
   812	          if (S->verb) { printf ("c ignoring deletion instruction %li: ", (lemmas - S->DB)); printClause (lemmas); }
   813	//        if (S->mode == BACKWARD_UNSAT) { // ignore pseudo unit clause deletion
   814	          S->proof[step] = 0; }
   815	        else { // if (S->mode == FORWARD_SAT) { // also for FORWARD_UNSAT?
   816	          removeWatch (S, lemmas, 0), removeWatch (S, lemmas, 1);
   817	          propagateUnits (S, 0); } }
   818	      else {
   819	        removeWatch (S, lemmas, 0), removeWatch (S, lemmas, 1); }
   820	      if (S->mode == FORWARD_UNSAT ) continue;   // Ignore deletion of top-level units
   821	      if (S->mode == BACKWARD_UNSAT) continue; }
   822	
   823	    int size = sortSize (S, lemmas); // after removal of watches
   824	
   825	    if (d && S->mode == FORWARD_SAT) {
   826	      if (size == -1) propagateUnits (S, 0);  // necessary?
   827	      if (redundancyCheck (S, lemmas, size, 1) == FAILED)  {
   828	        printf ("c failed at proof line %i (modulo deletion errors)\n", step + 1);
   829	        return SAT; }
   830	      continue; }
   831	
   832	    if (d == 0 && S->mode == FORWARD_UNSAT) {
   833	      if (step > end) {
   834	        if (size < 0) continue; // Fix of bus error: 10
   835	        if (redundancyCheck (S, lemmas, size, 1) == FAILED) {
   836	          printf ("c failed at proof line %i (modulo deletion errors)\n", step + 1);
   837	          return SAT; }
   838	
   839	        size = sortSize (S, lemmas);
   840	        S->nDependencies = 0; } }
   841	
   842	    if (lemmas[1])
   843	      addWatch (S, lemmas, 0), addWatch (S, lemmas, 1);
   844	
   845	    if (size == 0) { printf ("\rc conflict claimed, but not detected\n"); return SAT; }  // change to FAILED?
   846	    if (size == 1) {
   847	      if (S->verb) printf ("\rc found unit %i\n", lemmas[0]);
   848	      assign (S, lemmas[0]); S->reason[abs (lemmas[0])] = ((long) ((lemmas)-S->DB)) + 1;
   849	      if (propagate (S, 1, 1) == UNSAT) goto start_verification;
   850	      S->forced = S->processed; } }
   851	
   852	  if (S->mode == FORWARD_SAT && active == 0) {
   853	    postprocess (S); return UNSAT; }
   854	
   855	  if (S->mode == FORWARD_UNSAT) {
