 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Independent encoder M2 for one A2 skeleton case, written separately from encode_skel.py
     3	and the M1 helpers (no shared code). Input: a gen_skel_b skeleton (row types + #empty rows)
     4	and the multiset's special profiles.
     5	
     6	Rows: the given types (rows in >= 2 P0 quads) in the listed order, then for each P0 column v
     7	its 4 - deg(v) private rows, then the empty rows. Columns: P0 columns 0..c0-1, then the
     8	special columns in the order given. Cell variables v(r,c,s) = 1 + 48 r + 6 c + s.
     9	Clauses:
    10	  * one symbol per cell (ALO + pairwise AMO);
    11	  * P0 quad cells fixed (symbol 0 iff the row's type contains the column);
    12	  * every symbol s of column c at most n_{c,s} times (binomial (n+1)-subset clauses; the
    13	    counts sum to 19, so this forces exact counts);
    14	  * column-pair definition: no ordered symbol pair twice in any two columns (4-clauses);
    15	  * row identity (validity counting): with u(r,r') = 'rows r,r' agree nowhere' (forced true
    16	    when no agreement witness a(r,r',c,s) -> v(r,c,s) & v(r',c,s) is chosen),
    17	    sum_{r'} u(r,r') + sum_{special c} (n_{c,A[r][c]} - 2) = 2 - |T(r)| + #special,
    18	    encoded exactly with a totalizer over the multiset of literals (size-1 blocks moved to
    19	    the other side as negated literals);
    20	  * symmetry: in each P0 column c the rows of Q_d minus Q_c (d maximizing that set) get triple
    21	    labels 1,2,3(,4) in row order; in each special column, equal-count labels s < s+1 obey
    22	    first-occurrence order (long clauses); identical consecutive special columns are
    23	    lexicographically non-decreasing read top-down (order literals). The last two are
    24	    lex-leader constraints for the column-major reading order of the special columns; the P0
    25	    relabeling acts on other columns, so all hold simultaneously for some image of every
    26	    valid array. No row symmetry breaking.
    27	"""
    28	import itertools
    29	
    30	
    31	class Builder:
    32	    def __init__(self, nfixed):
    33	        self.nv = nfixed
    34	        self.cl = []
    35	
    36	    def new(self):
    37	        self.nv += 1
    38	        return self.nv
    39	
    40	    def totalizer(self, lits):
    41	        """unary sum: returns outputs o[0..n-1] with o[j] <-> (sum >= j+1)."""
    42	        layer = [[l] for l in lits]
    43	        if not layer:
    44	            return []
    45	        while len(layer) > 1:
    46	            nxt = []
    47	            for i in range(0, len(layer) - 1, 2):
    48	                a, b = layer[i], layer[i + 1]
    49	                o = [self.new() for _ in range(len(a) + len(b))]
    50	                for x in range(len(a) + 1):
    51	                    for y in range(len(b) + 1):
    52	                        if x + y == 0:
    53	                            continue
    54	                        c = [o[x + y - 1]]
    55	                        if x:
    56	                            c.append(-a[x - 1])
    57	                        if y:
    58	                            c.append(-b[y - 1])
    59	                        self.cl.append(c)
    60	                for x in range(len(a) + 1):
    61	                    for y in range(len(b) + 1):
    62	                        if x + y >= len(a) + len(b):
    63	                            continue
    64	                        c = [-o[x + y]]
    65	                        if x < len(a):
    66	                            c.append(a[x])
    67	                        if y < len(b):
    68	                            c.append(b[y])
    69	                        self.cl.append(c)
    70	                nxt.append(o)
    71	            if len(layer) % 2:
    72	                nxt.append(layer[-1])
    73	            layer = nxt
    74	        return layer[0]
    75	
    76	
    77	def build(c0, types, empty, special, N=19, K=8, Q=6):
    78	    assert N == 3 * Q + 1
    79	    deg = [0] * c0
    80	    for t in types:
    81	        for x in t:
    82	            deg[x] += 1
    83	    member = [set(t) for t in types]
    84	    for x in range(c0):
    85	        member += [{x} for _ in range(4 - deg[x])]
    86	    member += [set() for _ in range(empty)]
    87	    assert len(member) == N
    88	    profs = [[4] + [3] * (Q - 1)] * c0 + [sorted(p, reverse=True) for p in special]
    89	    assert len(profs) == K
    90	    v = lambda r, c, s: 1 + K * Q * r + Q * c + s  # noqa: E731
    91	    B = Builder(N * K * Q)
    92	    cl = B.cl
    93	    for r in range(N):
    94	        for c in range(K):
    95	            cl.append([v(r, c, s) for s in range(Q)])
    96	            for s, t in itertools.combinations(range(Q), 2):
    97	                cl.append([-v(r, c, s), -v(r, c, t)])
    98	            if c < c0:
    99	                cl.append([v(r, c, 0)] if c in member[r] else [-v(r, c, 0)])
   100	    for c in range(K):
   101	        for s in range(Q):
   102	            if c < c0 and s == 0:
   103	                continue
   104	            n = profs[c][s]
   105	            for sub in itertools.combinations(range(N), n + 1):
   106	                cl.append([-v(r, c, s) for r in sub])
   107	    for c1, c2 in itertools.combinations(range(K), 2):
   108	        for a in range(Q):
   109	            for b in range(Q):
   110	                for r, r2 in itertools.combinations(range(N), 2):
   111	                    cl.append([-v(r, c1, a), -v(r, c2, b), -v(r2, c1, a), -v(r2, c2, b)])
   112	    U = {}
   113	    for r, r2 in itertools.combinations(range(N), 2):
   114	        u = B.new()
   115	        U[(r, r2)] = u
   116	        wit = []
   117	        for c in range(K):
   118	            for s in range(Q):
   119	                a = B.new()
   120	                cl.append([-a, v(r, c, s)])
   121	                cl.append([-a, v(r2, c, s)])
   122	                wit.append(a)
   123	        cl.append(wit + [u])
   124	    ns = K - c0
   125	    for r in range(N):
   126	        lits = [U[(min(r, x), max(r, x))] for x in range(N) if x != r]
   127	        target = (N - 1) - 2 * c0 - ns - len(member[r])  # = 2 - |T| + ns when N=19, K=8
   128	        for c in range(c0, K):
   129	            for s in range(Q):
   130	                n = profs[c][s]
   131	                if n >= 2:
   132	                    lits += [v(r, c, s)] * (n - 2)
   133	                else:  # n == 1: coefficient -1 -> +(not v) on the left, +1 on the right
   134	                    lits.append(-v(r, c, s))
   135	                    target += 1
   136	        o = B.totalizer(lits)
   137	        if target < 0 or target > len(o):
   138	            cl.append([])
   139	            continue
   140	        if target >= 1:
   141	            cl.append([o[target - 1]])
   142	        if target < len(o):
   143	            cl.append([-o[target]])
   144	    for c in range(c0):
   145	        Qc = {r for r in range(N) if c in member[r]}
   146	        best = None
   147	        for d in range(c0):
   148	            if d == c:
   149	                continue
   150	            S = [r for r in range(N) if d in member[r] and r not in Qc]
   350	        for d in range(c0):
   351	            if d == c:
   352	                continue
   353	            S_ = [r for r in range(N) if d in member[r] and r not in Qc]
   354	            if best is None or len(S_) > len(best):
   355	                best = S_
   356	        if best:
   357	            for lab, r in enumerate(sorted(best), start=1):
   358	                cl.append([v(r, c, lab)])
   359	    for c in range(c0, K):
   360	        if c < c0 + m:
   361	            prs = [(s, s + 1) for s in range(2, Q - 2)]
   362	        else:
   363	            prs = [(s, s + 1) for s in range(Q - 1) if profs[c][s] == profs[c][s + 1]]
   364	        for s, s2 in prs:
   365	            for r in range(N):
   366	                cl.append([-v(r, c, s2)] + [v(r2, c, s) for r2 in range(r)])
   367	    for c in range(c0 + m, K - 1):
   368	        c2 = c + 1
   369	        eq_prev = None
   370	        for r in range(N):
   371	            for k in range(1, Q):
   372	                for s in range(k, Q):
   373	                    cl.append(([-eq_prev] if eq_prev else []) + [-v(r, c, s)] + [v(r, c2, t) for t in range(k, Q)])
   374	            if r < N - 1:
   375	                e = B.new()
   376	                for s in range(Q):
   377	                    cl.append(([-eq_prev] if eq_prev else []) + [-v(r, c, s), -v(r, c2, s), e])
   378	                eq_prev = e
   379	    return B.nv, cl
   380

   382	def build_partbatch(c0, types, empty, special, placements, N=19, K=8, Q=6):
   383	    """M2 batch of one-column placements: selector s_i per placement, clause OR s_i; the
   384	    first special column c* has exact label counts (binomial, as in build()) and, guarded by
   385	    s_i, its A rows -> 0, B rows -> 1, P rows -> Q-1 and the labels 0, 1, Q-1 excluded for all
   386	    other rows. Symmetry: P0 triple-label fixing; first-occurrence order among the triple
   387	    labels of c* and among equal-count labels of the free special columns; lexicographic
   388	    order between consecutive identical FREE special columns (column-major lex-leader).
   389	    UNSAT iff every placement subcase is UNSAT."""
   390	    placements = [pl[0] if len(pl) == 1 else pl for pl in placements]
   391	    deg = [0] * c0
   392	    for t in types:
   393	        for x in t:
   394	            deg[x] += 1
   395	    member = [set(t) for t in types]
   396	    for x in range(c0):
   397	        member += [{x} for _ in range(4 - deg[x])]
   398	    member += [set() for _ in range(empty)]
   399	    assert len(member) == N
   400	    profs = [[4] + [3] * (Q - 1)] * c0 + [sorted(p, reverse=True) for p in special]
   401	    assert all(p == [4, 4] + [3] * (Q - 3) + [2] for p in profs[c0:])
   402	    PAIR = Q - 1
   403	    cs = c0
   404	    v = lambda r, c, s: 1 + K * Q * r + Q * c + s  # noqa: E731
   405	    B = Builder(N * K * Q)
   406	    cl = B.cl
   407	    for r in range(N):
   408	        for c in range(K):
   409	            cl.append([v(r, c, s) for s in range(Q)])
   410	            for s, t in itertools.combinations(range(Q), 2):
   411	                cl.append([-v(r, c, s), -v(r, c, t)])
   412	            if c < c0:
   413	                cl.append([v(r, c, 0)] if c in member[r] else [-v(r, c, 0)])
   414	    for c in range(K):
   415	        for s in range(Q):
   416	            if c < c0 and s == 0:
   417	                continue
   418	            for sub in itertools.combinations(range(N), profs[c][s] + 1):
   419	                cl.append([-v(r, c, s) for r in sub])
   420	    sels = [B.new() for _ in placements]
   421	    cl.append(list(sels))
   422	    for s_i, (A, Bl, P) in zip(sels, placements):
   423	        role = {}
   424	        for r in A:
   425	            role[r] = 0
   426	        for r in Bl:
   427	            role[r] = 1
   428	        for r in P:
   429	            role[r] = PAIR
   430	        for r in range(N):
   431	            if r in role:
   432	                cl.append([-s_i, v(r, cs, role[r])])
   433	            else:
   434	                for s in (0, 1, PAIR):
   435	                    cl.append([-s_i, -v(r, cs, s)])
   436	    for c1, c2 in itertools.combinations(range(K), 2):
   437	        for a in range(Q):
   438	            for b in range(Q):
   439	                for r, r2 in itertools.combinations(range(N), 2):
   440	                    cl.append([-v(r, c1, a), -v(r, c2, b), -v(r2, c1, a), -v(r2, c2, b)])
   441	    U = {}
   442	    for r, r2 in itertools.combinations(range(N), 2):
   443	        u = B.new()
   444	        U[(r, r2)] = u
   445	        wit = []
   446	        for c in range(K):
   447	            for s in range(Q):
   448	                a = B.new()
   449	                cl.append([-a, v(r, c, s)])
   450	                cl.append([-a, v(r2, c, s)])
   451	                wit.append(a)
   452	        cl.append(wit + [u])
   453	    ns = K - c0
   454	    for r in range(N):
   455	        lits = [U[(min(r, x), max(r, x))] for x in range(N) if x != r]
   456	        target = (N - 1) - 2 * c0 - ns - len(member[r])
   457	        for c in range(c0, K):
   458	            for s in range(Q):
   459	                lits += [v(r, c, s)] * (profs[c][s] - 2)
   460	        o = B.totalizer(lits)
   461	        if target < 0 or target > len(o):
   462	            cl.append([])
   463	            continue
   464	        if target >= 1:
   465	            cl.append([o[target - 1]])
   466	        if target < len(o):
   467	            cl.append([-o[target]])
   468	    for c in range(c0):
   469	        Qc = {r for r in range(N) if c in member[r]}
   470	        best = None
   471	        for d in range(c0):
   472	            if d == c:
   473	                continue
   474	            S_ = [r for r in range(N) if d in member[r] and r not in Qc]
   475	            if best is None or len(S_) > len(best):
   476	                best = S_
   477	        if best:
   478	            for lab, r in enumerate(sorted(best), start=1):
   479	                cl.append([v(r, c, lab)])
   480	    for c in range(c0, K):
   481	        if c == cs:
   482	            prs = [(s, s + 1) for s in range(2, Q - 2)]
   483	        else:
   484	            prs = [(s, s + 1) for s in range(Q - 1) if profs[c][s] == profs[c][s + 1]]
   485	        for s, s2 in prs:
   486	            for r in range(N):
   487	                cl.append([-v(r, c, s2)] + [v(r2, c, s) for r2 in range(r)])
   488	    for c in range(cs + 1, K - 1):
   489	        c2 = c + 1
   490	        eq_prev = None
   491	        for r in range(N):
   492	            for k in range(1, Q):
   493	                for s in range(k, Q):
   494	                    cl.append(([-eq_prev] if eq_prev else []) + [-v(r, c, s)] + [v(r, c2, t) for t in range(k, Q)])
   495	            if r < N - 1:
   496	                e = B.new()
   497	                for s in range(Q):
   498	                    cl.append(([-eq_prev] if eq_prev else []) + [-v(r, c, s), -v(r, c2, s), e])
   499	                eq_prev = e
   500	    return B.nv, cl
   501	
   502	
   503	def build_v2(c0, types, empty, special, N=19, K=8, Q=6):
   504	    """M2 encoder version 2 (adds row symmetry breaking). Same clause families as build()
   505	    except the symmetry breaking, which is now entirely lex-leader for ONE row-major reading
   506	    order (rows top-down, columns left to right):
   507	      * P0 columns: first-occurrence order of the triple labels 1..Q-1 (replaces the label
   508	        fixing of build(), which is not a lex-leader constraint);
   509	      * special columns: first-occurrence order among equal-count labels;
   510	      * identical consecutive special columns: column read top-down lexicographically
   511	        non-decreasing (the row-major lex-leader constraint of the column swap);
   512	      * rows with identical P0 membership (same type), consecutive in the row order:
   513	        row r lexicographically <= row r' (the lex-leader constraint of the transposition),
   514	        encoded with order literals ge(r,c,k) <-> value >= k and prefix-equality literals.
   515	    The lex-least array of each orbit under the group generated by these symmetries satisfies
   516	    all of them."""
   517	    deg = [0] * c0
   518	    for t in types:
   519	        for x in t:
   520	            deg[x] += 1
   521	    member = [set(t) for t in types]
   522	    for x in range(c0):
   523	        member += [{x} for _ in range(4 - deg[x])]
   524	    member += [set() for _ in range(empty)]
   525	    assert len(member) == N
   526	    profs = [[4] + [3] * (Q - 1)] * c0 + [sorted(p, reverse=True) for p in special]
   527	    v = lambda r, c, s: 1 + K * Q * r + Q * c + s  # noqa: E731
   528	    B = Builder(N * K * Q)
   529	    cl = B.cl
   530	    for r in range(N):
   531	        for c in range(K):
   532	            cl.append([v(r, c, s) for s in range(Q)])
   533	            for s, t in itertools.combinations(range(Q), 2):
   534	                cl.append([-v(r, c, s), -v(r, c, t)])
   535	            if c < c0:
   536	                cl.append([v(r, c, 0)] if c in member[r] else [-v(r, c, 0)])
   537	    for c in range(K):
   538	        for s in range(Q):
   539	            if c < c0 and s == 0:
   540	                continue
   541	            for sub in itertools.combinations(range(N), profs[c][s] + 1):
   542	                cl.append([-v(r, c, s) for r in sub])
   543	    for c1, c2 in itertools.combinations(range(K), 2):
   544	        for a in range(Q):
   545	            for b in range(Q):
   546	                for r, r2 in itertools.combinations(range(N), 2):
   547	                    cl.append([-v(r, c1, a), -v(r, c2, b), -v(r2, c1, a), -v(r2, c2, b)])
   548	    U = {}
   549	    for r, r2 in itertools.combinations(range(N), 2):
   550	        u = B.new()
   551	        U[(r, r2)] = u
   552	        wit = []
   553	        for c in range(K):
   554	            for s in range(Q):
   555	                a = B.new()
   556	                cl.append([-a, v(r, c, s)])
   557	                cl.append([-a, v(r2, c, s)])
   558	                wit.append(a)
   559	        cl.append(wit + [u])
   560	    ns = K - c0
   561	    for r in range(N):
   562	        lits = [U[(min(r, x), max(r, x))] for x in range(N) if x != r]
   563	        target = (N - 1) - 2 * c0 - ns - len(member[r])
   564	        for c in range(c0, K):
   565	            for s in range(Q):
   680	        cl.append(wit + [u])
   681	    ns = K - c0
   682	    for r in range(N):
   683	        lits = [U[(min(r, x), max(r, x))] for x in range(N) if x != r]
   684	        target = (N - 1) - 2 * c0 - ns - len(member[r])
   685	        for c in range(c0, K):
   686	            for s in range(Q):
   687	                n = profs[c][s]
   688	                if n >= 2:
   689	                    lits += [v(r, c, s)] * (n - 2)
   690	                else:
   691	                    lits.append(-v(r, c, s))
   692	                    target += 1
   693	        o = B.totalizer(lits)
   694	        if target < 0 or target > len(o):
   695	            cl.append([])
   696	            continue
   697	        if target >= 1:
   698	            cl.append([o[target - 1]])
   699	        if target < len(o):
   700	            cl.append([-o[target]])
   701	    # first-occurrence order (P0 triple labels; equal-count special labels)
   702	    for c in range(K):
   703	        if c < c0:
   704	            prs = [(s, s + 1) for s in range(1 if base_quad else 0, Q - 1)]
   705	        else:
   706	            prs = [(s, s + 1) for s in range(Q - 1) if profs[c][s] == profs[c][s + 1]]
   707	        for s, s2 in prs:
   708	            for r in range(N):
   709	                cl.append([-v(r, c, s2)] + [v(r2, c, s) for r2 in range(r)])
   710	    # identical consecutive special columns: top-down lex
   711	    for c in range(c0, K - 1):
   712	        c2 = c + 1
   713	        if profs[c] != profs[c2]:
   714	            continue
   715	        eq_prev = None
   716	        for r in range(N):
   717	            for k in range(1, Q):
   718	                for s in range(k, Q):
   719	                    cl.append(([-eq_prev] if eq_prev else []) + [-v(r, c, s)] + [v(r, c2, t) for t in range(k, Q)])
   720	            if r < N - 1:
   721	                e = B.new()
   722	                for s in range(Q):
   723	                    cl.append(([-eq_prev] if eq_prev else []) + [-v(r, c, s), -v(r, c2, s), e])
   724	                eq_prev = e
   725	    # rows with identical type, consecutive: row lex (columns left to right)
   726	    for r in range(N - 1):
   727	        r2 = r + 1
   728	        if member[r] != member[r2] or len(member[r]) >= 2:
   729	            continue
   730	        eq_prev = None
   731	        for c in range(K):
   732	            for k in range(1, Q):
   733	                for s in range(k, Q):
   734	                    cl.append(([-eq_prev] if eq_prev else []) + [-v(r, c, s)] + [v(r2, c, t) for t in range(k, Q)])
   735	            if c < K - 1:
   736	                e = B.new()
   737	                for s in range(Q):
   738	                    cl.append(([-eq_prev] if eq_prev else []) + [-v(r, c, s), -v(r2, c, s), e])
   739	                eq_prev = e
   740	    return B.nv, cl

codex
For a complete binary proof, the checker’s byte count matches the file length; the cited truncated-CNF path is also rejected by the predicate. The remaining verdict race is concrete: cancellation can latch during the terminal gate or the later clock call, yet the runner can still write UNSAT/count=true before checking cancellation again.

   351	
   352	    def __init__(self, argv, out_path, cap):
   353	        self.argv, self.out_path, self.cap = argv, out_path, cap
   354	        self.captured, self.over_cap, self.reader_error = 0, False, None
   355	        self.term_sent_at, self.stop = None, None
   356	        self.sink = open(out_path, "xb")  # exclusive capture file BEFORE the child exists
   357	        try:
   358	            self.proc = subprocess.Popen(argv, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
   359	                                         stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, start_new_session=True,
   360	                                         close_fds=True, cwd=LANE + "/tmp", env=CHILD_ENV)
   361	        except BaseException:
   362	            self.sink.close()
   363	            raise
   364	        self.pid = self.pgid = self.proc.pid
   365	        self.started = time.time()
   366	        try:
   367	            self.identity_at_start = identity(self.pid)
   368	            self.reader = threading.Thread(target=self._read, daemon=True)
   369	            self.reader.start()
   370	        except BaseException:
   371	            # never leave an owned child unsupervised: one SIGTERM to the unreaped child, then wait
   372	            self.proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)
   373	            self.proc.wait()
   374	            self.sink.close()
   375	            raise
   376	
   377	    def _read(self):
   378	        try:
   379	            while True:
   380	                b = self.proc.stdout.read1(65536)
   381	                if not b:
   382	                    break
   383	                room = self.cap - self.captured
   384	                if room > 0:
   385	                    self.sink.write(b[:room])
   386	                self.captured += len(b)
   387	                if self.captured > self.cap:
   388	                    self.over_cap = True
   389	        except Exception as exc:
   390	            self.reader_error = repr(exc)
   391	        finally:
   392	            try:
   393	                self.sink.flush()
   394	                os.fsync(self.sink.fileno())
   395	                self.sink.close()
   396	                self.proc.stdout.close()
   397	            except Exception as exc:
   398	                self.reader_error = (self.reader_error or "") + " close:" + repr(exc)
   399	
   400	    def alive(self):
   401	        return self.proc.poll() is None
   402	
   403	    def term(self, reason):
   404	        if self.term_sent_at is None and self.proc.returncode is None:
   405	            self.stop = reason
   406	            self.term_sent_at = time.time()
   407	            self.proc.send_signal(signal.SIGTERM)  # Popen polls first; never signals a reaped PID
   408	
   409	    def settle(self, out_dir, label):
   410	        """Explicit wait; after the single SIGTERM no further signal is ever sent."""
   411	        quarantine = None
   412	        if self.term_sent_at is not None:
   413	            deadline = self.term_sent_at + TERM_WAIT
   414	            while self.proc.poll() is None and time.time() < deadline:
   415	                time.sleep(0.1)
   416	            if self.proc.poll() is None:
   417	                quarantine = os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".QUARANTINE.json")
   418	                write_x(quarantine, {"host_clock": clock(), "pid": self.pid, "pgid": self.pgid,
   419	                                     "argv": self.argv, "note": "survived SIGTERM + 60 s; no further signal; "
   420	                                     "runner keeps waiting and starts no new child"})
   421	        rc = self.proc.wait()
   422	        self.reader.join()
   423	        try:
   424	            os.killpg(self.pgid, 0)
   425	            group_empty = False
   426	        except ProcessLookupError:
   427	            group_empty = True
   428	        except PermissionError:
   429	            group_empty = False
   430	        return {"pid": self.pid, "pgid": self.pgid, "identity_at_start": self.identity_at_start,
   431	                "argv": self.argv, "returncode": rc, "stop": self.stop or "self",
   432	                "seconds": round(time.time() - self.started, 3), "captured_bytes": self.captured,
   433	                "capture_over_cap": self.over_cap, "reader_error": self.reader_error,
   434	                "group_empty_after_reap": group_empty, "quarantine_record": quarantine,
   435	                "output_path": self.out_path, "output_sha256": sha_file(self.out_path)[0]}
   444	def supervise(child, wall, sink):
   445	    """Monitor until exit or a single trip; any monitor exception is itself a trip."""
   446	    me = os.getpid()
   447	    last_rss = last_reg = 0.0
   448	    peak = 0
   449	    while child.alive():
   450	        try:
   451	            now = time.time()
   452	            if CANCEL.is_set():
   453	                child.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
   454	            elif now - child.started > wall + GRACE:
   455	                child.term("runner_wall_bound")
   456	            elif sink is not None and (sink.over_cap or sink.error):
   457	                child.term("proof_cap" if sink.over_cap else "proof_sink_error")
   458	            elif child.over_cap:
   459	                child.term("capture_cap")
   460	            elif free_bytes() < FREE_FLOOR:
   461	                child.term("inflight_disk_floor")
   462	            if child.term_sent_at is None and now - last_rss >= 1.0:
   463	                last_rss = now
   464	                r, n = rss_bytes([me, child.pid])
   465	                peak = max(peak, r)
   466	                if n < 2 and child.alive():
   467	                    child.term("rss_unreadable")
   468	                elif r > LANE_RSS_TRIP:
   469	                    child.term("lane_rss")
   470	            if child.term_sent_at is None and now - last_reg >= 5.0:
   471	                last_reg = now
   472	                if registry_refusals():
   473	                    child.term("stop_registry")
   474	        except Exception as exc:
   475	            child.term("monitor_exception:%r" % exc)
   476	        if child.term_sent_at is not None:
   477	            break
   478	        time.sleep(0.25)
   479	    return child.stop, peak

   481	
   482	def run_child(argv, out_dir, label, wall, sink=None):
   483	    ch = Child(argv, os.path.join(out_dir, label + ".out"), LOG_CAP)
   484	    try:
   485	        if CANCEL.is_set():
   486	            ch.term("owner_cancel_sigterm")
   487	        trip, peak = supervise(ch, wall, sink)
   488	    except BaseException as exc:
   489	        ch.term("runner_exception:%r" % exc)
   490	        trip, peak = ch.stop, None
   491	    finally:
   492	        rec = ch.settle(out_dir, label)
   493	    rec["trip"] = trip
   494	    rec["peak_sampled_lane_rss_bytes"] = peak
   495	    return rec, segments(ch.out_path)

   498	def run_kissat(cnf_path, proof_file, out_dir, label, seconds, proof_cap=PROOF_CAP):
   499	    """kissat with its proof routed through a hard-capped owned sink; proof_file None = no proof."""
   500	    argv = [KISSAT[0], "-q", "-f", "--seed=1", "--time=%d" % seconds, cnf_path]
   501	    sink = None
   502	    if proof_file is not None:
   503	        fifo_dir = os.path.join(out_dir, "fifo")
   504	        os.makedirs(fifo_dir, exist_ok=True)
   505	        sink = ProofSink(os.path.join(fifo_dir, label + ".proof.fifo"), proof_file, proof_cap)
   506	        argv.append(sink.fifo_path)
   507	    try:
   508	        rec, lines = run_child(argv, out_dir, label + ".kissat", seconds, sink)
   509	    finally:
   510	        sink_rec = sink.finish() if sink is not None else None
   511	    rec["proof_sink"] = sink_rec
   512	    return rec, lines

   646	    cnf2 = os.path.join(d, "php6x5-truncated.cnf")
   647	    write_bytes_x(cnf2, trunc)
   648	    dr2, dl2 = run_drat(cnf2, proof, d, "C2", 60)
   649	    c2 = qualify_unsat(exp, ks, kl, ph, pn, dr2, dl2, (h, n), (ph, pn))
   650	    recs["C2_truncated_cnf"] = {"intended": "REJECT: early EOF or proof never consumed", "defects": c2, "drat": dr2}
   651	    ok &= any(("early EOF" in x) or ("clauses less than expected" in x) or ("consumed" in x) for x in c2)
   652	    # C3: proof with one trailing garbage byte -> consumed bytes != file bytes (wrong binary prefix).
   653	    with open(proof, "rb") as f:
   654	        pbytes = f.read()
   655	    proof3 = os.path.join(d, "php6x5-trailing.drat")
   656	    p3h, p3n = write_bytes_x(proof3, pbytes + b"x")
   657	    dr3, dl3 = run_drat(cnf, proof3, d, "C3", 60)
   658	    c3 = qualify_unsat(exp, ks, kl, p3h, p3n, dr3, dl3, (h, n), (p3h, p3n))
   659	    recs["C3_trailing_proof_byte"] = {"intended": "REJECT: consumed bytes / binary prefix", "defects": c3, "drat": dr3}
   660	    ok &= any(("consumed" in x) or ("wrong binary prefix" in x) for x in c3)
   661	    # C4: satisfiable formula (PHP 5x5) checked against the PHP 6x5 proof -> rejection.
   662	    raw4 = php_cnf(5, 5)
   663	    cnf4 = os.path.join(d, "php5x5-sat.cnf")
   664	    h4, n4 = write_bytes_x(cnf4, raw4)
   665	    exp4 = (h4, n4, raw4.split(b"\n", 1)[0].decode())
   666	    dr4, dl4 = run_drat(cnf4, proof, d, "C4", 60)
   667	    c4 = qualify_unsat(exp4, ks, kl, ph, pn, dr4, dl4, (h4, n4), (ph, pn))
   668	    recs["C4_sat_formula_foreign_proof"] = {"intended": "REJECT", "defects": c4, "drat": dr4}
   669	    ok &= (c4 != [])
   670	    # C5: HARD proof cap 4096 B on a hard instance -> sink never writes beyond 4096, trip proof_cap, settled.
   671	    cnf5 = os.path.join(d, "php11x10.cnf")
   672	    write_bytes_x(cnf5, php_cnf(11, 10))
   673	    proof5 = os.path.join(d, "php11x10.drat")
   674	    ks5, kl5 = run_kissat(cnf5, proof5, d, "C5", 60, proof_cap=4096)
   675	    size5 = os.lstat(proof5).st_size
   676	    recs["C5_proof_hard_cap"] = {"intended": "trip=proof_cap, file<=4096 B, reaped, group empty, no quarantine",
   677	                                 "kissat": ks5, "proof_file_bytes": size5}
   678	    ok &= (ks5["trip"] == "proof_cap" and size5 <= 4096 and ks5["group_empty_after_reap"] is True
   679	           and not ks5["quarantine_record"] and ks5["proof_sink"]["over_cap"] is True
   680	           and not ks5["proof_sink"]["thread_alive_after_join"])
   681	    # C6: native solver time limit, no proof file -> self exit without an UNSAT verdict.
   682	    cnf6 = os.path.join(d, "php13x12.cnf")
   683	    write_bytes_x(cnf6, php_cnf(13, 12))
   684	    ks6, kl6 = run_kissat(cnf6, None, d, "C6", 1)
   685	    recs["C6_native_time_limit"] = {"intended": "self exit, no s UNSATISFIABLE", "kissat": ks6,
   686	                                    "status_lines": [s for s in kl6 if s.startswith("s ")]}
   687	    ok &= (ks6["trip"] is None and ks6["returncode"] != 20 and ks6["group_empty_after_reap"] is True
   688	           and "s UNSATISFIABLE" not in kl6)
   689	    # C7: unreadable input (path does not exist) with a proof sink -> no verdict, sink settles, no hang.
   690	    ks7, kl7 = run_kissat(os.path.join(d, "absent.cnf"), os.path.join(d, "absent.drat"), d, "C7", 10)
   691	    recs["C7_no_input_sink_liveness"] = {"intended": "no rc20, reaped, sink thread finished", "kissat": ks7}
   692	    ok &= (ks7["returncode"] != 20 and ks7["group_empty_after_reap"] is True
   693	           and not ks7["proof_sink"]["thread_alive_after_join"])
   694	    return ok, recs
   749	    def gate(projected, remaining_reserve):
   750	        why = registry_refusals()
   751	        fb = free_bytes()
   752	        if fb < FRESH_BASE + projected:
   753	            why.append(f"fresh free {fb} < 12.2e9 + {projected}")
   754	        if fb - remaining_reserve < FREE_FLOOR:
   755	            why.append(f"free {fb} - remaining reservations {remaining_reserve} < 12e9")
   756	        if time.time() + ITEM_WORST_SECONDS > min(abs_end, OWNED_SETTLED_BY - 180):
   757	            why.append("chunk-fit/absolute deadline")
   758	        if CANCEL.is_set():
   759	            why.append("owner cancel")
   760	        why += stability_refusals(projected)
   761	        return why, fb

   773	        ok, recs = controls(out_dir)

   822	            ks, kl = run_kissat(cnf_path, proof_path, out_dir, label, SOLVER_SECONDS)

   834	                return code
   835	            sink_ok = sink_complete(ks["proof_sink"])

   838	            if ks["returncode"] == 20 and ks["stop"] == "self" and ks["trip"] is None and not safety \

   847	                                               dr, dl, post_cnf, post_proof)

   849	                rec["terminal_gate"] = terminal_gate()

   857	            summary["items"][it["id"]] = {k: rec.get(k) for k in ("status", "count_for_scientific_coverage",

   223	def stability_refusals(projected):
   224	    """resource-stability-addendum v1 rule over the v2-pinned v3 monitor (P1)."""
   225	    st = os.lstat(MONITOR)
   226	    if not stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode):
   227	        return ["monitor is not a regular file"]
   228	    with open(MONITOR, "rb") as f:
   229	        raw = f.read()
   230	    complete = raw.split(b"\n")[:-1]  # the last element is b"" after a final newline or an ignored partial line
   231	    rows = []
   232	    for i, ln in enumerate(complete):
   233	        try:
   234	            o = json.loads(ln)
   235	            if not isinstance(o, dict):
   236	                raise ValueError("not an object")
   237	            if "event" in o:
   238	                if i == 0 and set(o) == START_KEYS and o["event"] == "observer_start" \
   239	                        and o["source_sha256"] == OBSERVER_SOURCE_SHA \
   240	                        and datetime.fromisoformat(o["at"]).tzinfo is not None:
   241	                    continue
   242	                return ["monitor event line %d not an allowed observer_start (or observer ended): %r" % (i, ln[:160])]
   243	            if set(o) != SAMPLE_KEYS or not isinstance(o["free"], int) or isinstance(o["free"], bool):
   244	                raise ValueError("sample schema")
   245	            dt = datetime.fromisoformat(o["t"])
   246	            if dt.tzinfo is None:
   247	                raise ValueError("naive timestamp")
   248	            rows.append((dt.timestamp(), o["free"]))
   249	        except Exception as exc:
   250	            return ["malformed monitor line %d: %r (%r)" % (i, ln[:160], exc)]
   251	    if not rows:
   252	        return ["no monitor samples"]
   253	    for a, b in zip(rows, rows[1:]):
   254	        if not b[0] > a[0]:
   255	            return ["monitor timestamps not strictly increasing"]
   256	    why = []
   257	    now = time.time()
   258	    t_last, free_last = rows[-1]
   259	    if not (0 <= now - t_last <= 30):
   260	        why.append(f"monitor last sample age {now - t_last:.1f}s outside [0,30]")
   261	    win = [r for r in rows if now - 300 <= r[0] <= now]
   262	    if len(win) < 25:
   263	        why.append(f"only {len(win)} samples in [now-300, now]")
   264	    elif win[-1][0] - win[0][0] < 290:
   265	        why.append(f"window span {win[-1][0] - win[0][0]:.1f}s < 290 s")
   266	    if free_last < 13_500_000_000 + projected:
   267	        why.append(f"last free {free_last} < 13.5e9 + projected {projected}")
   268	    allowed = max(300_000_000, (free_last - 13_500_000_000) // 12)
   269	    peak, drop = None, 0
   270	    for _, fr in win:
   271	        peak = fr if peak is None else max(peak, fr)
   272	        drop = max(drop, peak - fr)
   273	    if drop > allowed:
   274	        why.append(f"peak-to-later drop {drop} > allowed {allowed}")
   275	    return why
   325	    def finish(self):
   326	        """After the producer is reaped: unblock a never-connected reader, then bounded join."""
   327	        deadline = time.time() + 10
   328	        while self.thread.is_alive() and not self.connected and time.time() < deadline:
   329	            try:
   330	                fd = os.open(self.fifo_path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_NONBLOCK)
   331	                self.unblocked_by_runner = True
   332	                os.close(fd)
   333	                break
   334	            except OSError as exc:
   335	                if exc.errno != errno.ENXIO:
   336	                    self.error = (self.error or "") + " unblock:" + repr(exc)
   337	                    break
   338	                time.sleep(0.05)
   339	        self.thread.join(SINK_JOIN_SECONDS)
   340	        rec = {"fifo": self.fifo_path, "file": self.file_path, "cap": self.cap, "connected": self.connected,
   341	               "eof": self.eof, "received": self.received, "written": self.written, "discarded": self.discarded,
   342	               "over_cap": self.over_cap, "error": self.error, "unblocked_by_runner": self.unblocked_by_runner,
   343	               "thread_alive_after_join": self.thread.is_alive(), "sha256_of_written": self.sha.hexdigest()}

   345	            rec["file_rehash"] = list(sha_file(self.file_path))
   346	        return rec
